  E. Once resolved, update the actual rule, tests, UI explanation, set notes, version and history.

10.6 Required CHANGELOG entry template (append; don't erase history)
  Date: YYYY-MM-DD
  Document revision: x.y -> x.z
  App version / source baseline: v...
  Rule IDs changed: ...
  Status: [LOCKED]/[EXCEPTION]/[CHECK]/[BUG]
  User decision / verified card wording: ...
  Previous rule/implementation: ...
  New requirement / exception: ...
  Code locations: ...
  New/updated tests: ...
  Cross-set regression coverage: ...
  Android device QA: tested / not tested
  Known caveats / follow-up: ...

10.7 Single-file upkeep
  Keep this as one easy-to-read plain TXT file because the user uses new chats and phone uploads. Extra technical/per-set docs may exist, but they MUST link or point back to this file and must not quietly supersede its shared rules.
  Include this file in EVERY future rules-changing changed-files patch (for example under docs/LUCKYDEX_MASTER_RULES.txt).
  Do not increment the document revision just because a build number changes with no content change; DO append the expansion register/status note or 'no global rule changes' where needed.
  If requested, a short project-level prompt can point all future conversations to this file, but the full detail lives here rather than being limited by custom-instruction space.


10.8 [LOCKED] C-RELEASE-GATE-001 — SOURCE-TRUTH PREFLIGHT (NO GUESSES)
  At expansion start, identify and verify: exact ZIP/source commit and version; scripts/registries/picker/opponents; exact English card-print set ID, numbered count and secret/alternate prints; authority URL for EVERY printed card; pre-existing mechanics; cross-era conversion rules; intended release order and all required running assets. Read THIS FILE and the most recent postrelease audit before coding. Do not extrapolate printed rules from names or other expansions. Record baseline passing/failing tests before a change so old failures are not attributed to new work.
  Compare printed set record count against catalogue with exact number/ID continuity, names, type, stage, HP, evolved-from, Weakness and Resistance MAGNITUDE, retreat, rarity, image, attacks, costs, damage, ability name/type/trigger, Trainer/Stadium/Special Energy text and Basic Energy identity. Do NOT infer effect correctness from a card scan or an artwork URL.

10.9 [LOCKED] C-RELEASE-GATE-002 — EXPLICIT PER-CARD EXECUTION CONTRACT
  For EVERY attack, Power, Body, Trainer, Stadium, Tool or Energy effect write down:
    (a) acting/choosing side, victim side, and who may DECLINE; (b) exact trigger and
    play/evolution/attack/KO/retreat/between-turn window; (c) prior conditions and
    Energy costs, plus any additional discard, reveal or self-KO costs; (d) source,
    destination and ownership of each card/Energy/counter, Basic Zone vs physical
    hand/deck/discard, including special Energy; (e) choice order, number and
    eligible targets/hand positions; (f) hidden vs visible information; (g) timing
    of damage, Weakness, Resistance, prevention, healing, statuses and KO/Prizes;
    (h) exact duration and cleanup; (i) CPU strategy and touchscreen presentation.
  Trace real BEFORE and AFTER game state in tests. A registered handler, a no-op
  fallback, or merely opening a picker NEVER counts as a verified effect.
  Every optional 'may' starts with a real Use/Decline decision (player and CPU),
  and Decline must leave all source/destination zones unchanged. A forced action
  with a legal target must never quietly skip. Choice ownership MUST agree with
  printed text; card identity cannot be shown for facedown opponent hand picks.

10.10 [LOCKED] C-RELEASE-GATE-003 — INTERACTION DEPENDENCY MATRIX
  BEFORE enabling a card map both forward and reverse synergies across:
    Stadium <-> Power/Body/attack/status; Tool <-> attack/evolution/ability;
    Special Energy <-> costs/activation/KO/discard/retrieval; Supporter/Trainer
    <-> hand/deck/Zone/discard; Bench/Active/KO/retreat <-> triggered abilities;
    evolution stages <-> support Powers; card classifications (ex, Star, LV.X,
    delta, Dark, Rocket, Fossil) <-> limitations and printed interactions.
  Test an interaction WITH its required partner, WITHOUT the partner, and WITH
  the partner disabled/suppressed or out of range. Test opponent and player
  separately and verify the CPU makes a sensible choice. Example historical
  failures: Cacturne only works with Sidney's Stadium; Altaria heals under
  Drake's Stadium; Xatu must pair with a COMPLETE Stage 2 ex beneficiary;
  Power-disabled cards cannot silently trigger; Tool-demanding attacks need
  a legal Tool. Capture new global interactions in this file, not merely notes.

10.11 [LOCKED] C-RELEASE-GATE-004 — DECKBUILDER & CPU INVARIANTS
  For BOTH owned player decks and all-cards Test Lab/CPU, test:
  * Exactly 30 real cards, legal per-name copy cap, owned copies when relevant,
    legal species/form limits, allowed set/rarity/Stage restriction and one
    playable Basic; never pull a gated/unsupported card.
  * All selected attacking Pokemon can actually pay at least one useful attack
    from the computed Basic Energy Zone and permitted physical Special Energy;
    do not add off-type lines just because synergy metadata says 'preferred'.
  * If the user explicitly chooses ONE Energy type, only that type is planned;
    Colorless costs are payable, but no unrequested colored Energy type may
    leak in. Widen automatically ONLY if type selection was not locked and
    explain this in the returned plan.
  * Complete Basic->Stage 1->Stage 2 evolution chain where feasible; NEVER add
    a stranded Stage 1, a useless Pokémon-ex-only support or an out-of-set
    evolution. Baby ancestry and LV.X level-up are distinct from Stage 1/2;
    LV.X must retain its previous-level attacks and be legal only as printed.
  * Include real consumable Tools/Energy/Stadium/Bench partners for conditional
    cards. Support effects must change composition, not just appear as tags.
  * Focused build on every battle-enabled Pokémon with a four-copy synthetic
    collection: PASS, or provide a machine-testable explanation for impossible
    set/type/ownership constraints. Do not silently 'pass' an unbuilt focus.
  * Seeded CPU builds across ALL eight Basic Energy types, all nine strategy
    archetypes, single-set / selected mixed-set pools, with no strategy locks,
    blank actions, hidden-hand peeking, endless choice loops or habitual skips
    for beneficial effects. Explicitly report unsupported types while staged.

10.12 [LOCKED] C-RELEASE-GATE-005 — TESTS MUST DEMONSTRATE OUTCOMES
  Minimum release matrix for EACH new effect family:
    successful and ineligible use, no-target case, zero/multiple target case,
    mandatory and declined optional paths, player and CPU as actor, appropriate
    self/opponent ownership, attack/Bench/KO/checkpoint timing, stacked effects
    and ability suppression. Count real affected cards/counters, not only logs.
  For random effects fix RNG seeds and assert BOTH heads/tails branches.
  For search/discard/transfers assert the exact CARD ID and ZONE before/after;
  do not let card-count coincidences mask a wrong destination. For attacks,
  compare printed damage at base, Weakness/Resistance, protected Bench,
  multipliers/additive damage and copied attacks including non-Energy costs.
  Repeat an EX-era regression selection after cross-era shared-engine edits.
  Record any known baseline failures separately and never relabel them 'fixed'.
  Attack smoke tests are necessary but INSUFFICIENT: a no-crash attack can be
  entirely wrong; each unique effect family also requires outcome tests.

10.13 [LOCKED] C-RELEASE-GATE-006 — NON-NEGOTIABLE CARD LEDGER & RELEASE WORDING
  Keep one ledger row for EACH print with: ID/name, printed metadata verified
  (Y/N), attack handler tested (Y/N/N/A), activated/triggered ability tested,
  relevant dependency tested, owner/CPU choice tested, deckbuild tested, result
  FULL/PARTIAL/BLOCKED and the remaining defect. Source registration is NOT
  battle readiness, and battle readiness is NOT proof of print-level fidelity.
  Stage releases may list all cards in the collection but enable only cards
  with complete executable effects and corresponding tests. A required mechanic
  absent from the current engine remains BLOCKED with an explicit issue.
  NEVER say a set is 'complete' until ALL printed cards are battle-enabled,
  print-vs-runtime effects are individually audited, legal focused and CPU
  builds are verified or explained, and targeted plus cross-set tests pass.
  Even 'complete' means SOURCE completion only unless real Android touch QA,
  artwork and CPU matches were actually performed. Separate test classes,
  passing counts, skipped assertions, known limitations and user-visible steps.

10.14 [LOCKED] C-RELEASE-GATE-007 — HANDOFF AND UI
  Preserve prior versions, apply source-relative incremental patches and include
  this MASTER RULES file in every rules-changing ZIP. Increment app/versionCode,
  produce actual reproducible tests, verify ZIP paths and list required baseline.
  Prefer player card ART with no duplicate descriptive overlay where artwork
  loads; on failure show readable text. Facing-down card position choices must
  not reveal hidden card identities. The Android UI, CPU and Test Lab must use
  the SAME validated action/choice state machine, not separate placeholder logic.
  In release notes include: exact completed/gated counts, code changes, tested
  interaction matrix and matchups, full command/output summary, known gaps,
  install block, no false phone-test claim, next gated subset by card ID.

10.14A [LOCKED] C-RELEASE-GATE-008 — SMALL-BATCH OUTCOME AND EVOLUTION CERTIFICATION (v2.16)
  * Do not count a Stage 1 card as set-only focus-ready when its evolution is
    required by the complete-family policy but not yet enabled. An attack-smoke
    pass is NOT a focus-deck pass: independently test each stage in the allowed
    set/type pool with four owned copies and test the exact reason for failure.
  * For split evolutionary trees (Wurmple->Silcoon->Beautifly and
    Wurmple->Cascoon->Dustox) validate each COMPLETE playable branch with all
    required earlier stages, and never combine incompatible branches as one line.
  * Write assertions against ATTACK OUTPUT after weakness/resistance and target
    protection. A printed Bench 10 must not be mistaken for Active 10 when the
    Active is Lightning-weak (+10), and a defeated Active may be removed before
    reading its final damage: inspect KO log/discard rather than dereferencing it.
  * When an attack says '10 for each damage counter', remember battle-state
    damage is stored as HP units (damage 30 equals 3 counters, so bonus 30), not
    as a counter tally. Test injured AND uninjured attacker/defender cases.
  * Test protection on the next actual opponent attack, including a threshold
    hit and an above-threshold hit, AND with Weakness applied; creating an
    attackGuard or modifier object alone is not an outcome test.
  * On-card conditional healing such as Giga Drain/Leech Seed must use the
    damage actually dealt, not just printed attack damage; test nullified hits.
  * New attack target choices must be confirmed in the actual effect-choice
    state, including a Bench-selected path. Coin and status timing must be
    asserted in the right between-turn phase, never guessed from final status.
  * Reuse verified shared effects but record if a selected pool cannot produce
    a complete line; never un-gate a Pokémon just to satisfy a focus builder.

10.14B [LOCKED] C-RELEASE-GATE-009 — EFFECT DISPATCH AND CONDITIONAL SMOKE INPUTS (v2.17)
  * All new printed-effect rules must be registered in the correct dispatch
    chain and dispatch EXACT rule IDs before a broad set-prefix handler.
    Adding a generic dp1:* handler may silently mask previously working
    dp1:72:splashAbout or other older cases. Always rerun both preceding
    batches' specific outcome tests, not just the newest batch.
  * A 'can legally attack' smoke test must construct the *printed legal*
    target state first: Prinplup Brine requires an already damaged opposing
    Pokemon. Similarly, searches need eligible cards and a free Bench space;
    conditional attacks may require a status, Stadium, hand card or Energy.
    A missing prerequisite in a smoke test is not evidence of a card bug.
  * For attacks with an optional number of Bench targets, test selecting zero,
    one and multiple distinct eligible Pokemon. 'Choose up to two' must show
    an explicit decline path, never require two available targets, and still
    apply unrelated next-turn locks if targets are declined.
  * For deck search/attachment (Grotle Synthesis), separately test a chosen
    PHYSICAL Basic Energy drawn from the deck and the ordered Energy Zone:
    exactly one Energy arrives on the chosen target, the actual source loses
    one, and shuffle/visibility work as printed. CPU must not conjure a new
    Energy token or accept the wrong source type.
  * Damage-checkpoints, self-KO recoil, conditional statuses and next-turn
    restrictions require explicit log/state assertions. In particular, a
    self-knockout can remove the active slot, so examine the KO log, and an
    attack that does nothing against a currently awake defender must remain
    playable unless its printed text forbids announcing the attack.

10.14C [LOCKED] C-RELEASE-GATE-010 — FOCUSED ENERGY PLAN (v2.17)
  * A single-type focused deck must include a complete permitted evolution
    line AND at least one payable attack for every included stage. An older
    pre-evolution's OPTIONAL typed move is not an obligatory Energy type
    when it has a Colorless/otherwise payable attack. Example: Onix has
    Colorless Headbutt, so Steelix with Metal Energy alone is usable.
  * Never generalise this pruning to pre-DP sets without separate tests:
    older type-dependent partners, attack conditions and dual-type bonuses
    still require their own plan. Test selected-set filters for every line
    and give explicit errors if a higher evolution remains battle-gated.
  * CPU opponent-generation is a SEPARATE release gate from focused player
    decks. A focused Metal Steelix build does not prove the randomized
    DP-only Metal CPU generator can find a legal deck. Document both checks.

10.14D [LOCKED] C-RELEASE-GATE-011 — BABY EVOLUTION / EVOLUTION STACK DEPENDENCY (v2.18)
  * Never count a Baby Pokemon as an optional harmless tag when the evolved
    attack explicitly checks for that Baby beneath it. The focused auto
    builder must include the actual named Baby (Azurill/Marill/Azumarill,
    Budew/Roselia/Roserade) with a playable attack/evolution route.
    The relevant named Baby must exist and be battle-enabled in the chosen
    set. Do not introduce off-filter Energy to make a support Baby attack.
  * Baby Evolution is a once-per-turn, voluntary Poké-Power, not an
    ordinary Stage 1 play from hand: test offered/declined Power, source
    hand selection, correct matching evolution, preserved stack, healing,
    disabled-by-Condition, timing and CPU selection. Attack-level checks
    must independently verify both with-Baby and without-Baby outcomes.
  * All multi-target, Energy-count and between-turn damage examples must
    set up a non-weak target when the test is for raw damage and explicitly
    account for Poison/checkup ticks and Pokémon KOs.

10.14E [LOCKED] C-RELEASE-GATE-012 — TRAINER REPRINT OUTCOME AND ARTWORK (v2.18)
  * Matching an old rule ID is not enough to call a Trainer reprint done.
    Run actual hand -> play -> target -> destination -> end-state tests,
    including both coin outcomes and any opponent/owner choices.
  * When retrieving a Pokémon and attachments from a Super Scoop Up, the
    *whole* evolution stack, attached Special Energy, Tools and Basic Energy
    must return on heads; tails must leave that Pokémon untouched. For the
    DP1 reprint, synthetic recovered Basic Energy should use the matching
    DP1 printing rather than unrelated older-set artwork where identity is
    lost to the Energy Zone. Respect the application's hand limit.
  * Check selected source Pokémon, actual attached Basic Energy subtype,
    and different destination Pokémon for Energy Switch. The deckbuilder
    must preserve single-type restrictions even if a supporting Pokémon
    has another native type. The mono-Metal CPU generation gap from v0.69.225 was fixed in
    v0.69.226 by testing a payable attack at each included Pokemon; keep
    per-Energy CPU regressions to detect future recurrence.
  * In stage releases, report exact enabled/gated numbers and test results,
    and leave all nonimplemented LV.X and triggered Powers gated.

10.14F [LOCKED] C-RELEASE-GATE-013 — CHOICE PRIVACY, REAL TARGETS, REAL OUTCOMES (v2.19)
  * An effect that chooses an opposing hand card WITHOUT LOOKING must show
    facedown positions only, not card identities or a random chosen card.
    Test a known two-card hand, choose position 1, verify the exact physical
    card is moved to the correct discard/deck destination, and ensure
    the other card remains. Test the opponent/CPU side too.
  * Printed optional effects must offer both an actual decline and a legal
    accept target. Test both outcomes, not just that a popup or action
    exists. If a target requires attached Energy, an empty slot is excluded.
  * Printed BEFORE DAMAGE switching (e.g., DP Magneton Magnetic Ray) must
    change the active defender BEFORE base damage, Weakness, Resistance,
    damage prevention and KO evaluation. Test both choose and decline with
    distinct, Energy-qualified Bench targets; preserve defending side.
  * Trainer and Poké-Power coin outcomes must be tested independently using
    deterministic seeds; test the exact card/entity destination, the right
    source zone (hand, deck, ordinary discard, Basic-Energy ledger), and
    unchanged state when the effect is declined or its coin flip fails.

10.14G [LOCKED] C-RELEASE-GATE-014 — PRINTED STATUS TICK AMOUNTS (v2.19)
  * A printed stronger Poison (e.g., DP Skuntank Toxic Cloud, two damage
    counters) requires amount=20 in the status record; ordinary Poison is
    amount=10. Verify the between-turn tick with the proper phase and do
    not mistake automatic Sleep recovery or normal Weakness for an effect
    bug. Reversing the attack or target side must not alter the rule.
  * All self-damage, status, choice and Bench interaction smoke tests must
    include targeted, assertive outcome tests for any new shared handler.
    A validated effect object is only syntax, not proof of card correctness.

10.14H [LOCKED] C-RELEASE-GATE-015 — PER-POKEMON PAYABLE ATTACK / CPU ENERGY (v2.19)
  * A Pokemon may have a printed off-type attack but still be playable on a
    mono-Energy plan if at least one different attack is fully payable.
    DP Steelix/Onix is the canonical example: Onix's Colorless Headbutt
    works from Metal Energy and Steelix's Metal attacks work from Metal.
  * For DP1-only CPU builds, inspect every included Pokemon's attack costs,
    not a global union of every optional printed cost. Require a legal
    30-card deck, only permitted cards and Basic Energy, at least one
    payable attack per Pokemon, all expected evolution stages, and true
    matching of the selected type. Do not relax older-set Energy rules
    as an accidental side effect of this DP-specific repair.
  * Run seeded opponent builds for all eight Basic Energy types after each
    new release, especially Metal. A Colorless-only requested archetype
    needs a real Basic Energy type supply; it does not imply that a
    printed Colorless Basic Energy card exists.
  * Partner metadata must be checked for Baby Evolution STACK payoffs even
    when the partner is a Basic or Stage 1 rather than a final Stage 2:
    DP Electabuzz's Shock Wave needs Elekid, and Clefairy's Moon Impact
    needs Cleffa. Choose no off-filter Energy solely for support Babies.
  * All LV.X remain gated until their Active-only Level-Up legality,
    previous-level inherited attacks/Powers, interaction/retreat/KO/Prize
    timing and player/CPU choices are implemented and verified.

10.14I [LOCKED] C-RELEASE-GATE-016 — ABILITY LOCATION AND TWO-SIDED DECISIONS (v2.20)
  * Before enabling activated abilities, verify exactly which location the
    source must occupy (Active, Bench, either) and gate the action accordingly.
    DP Unown A/B/C/D Powers are Bench-only. Test the legal Bench activation,
    the illegal Active attempt, the once-per-turn limit, and both CPU sides.
  * A printed 'each player may' clause needs TWO independent, side-owned
    decisions; never infer that the opponent agrees because the attacker did.
    Present both accept and decline, preserve player-private information,
    and test the choices on either side of the battle.
  * If an optional effect moves the Active Pokémon out of play (including
    bouncing an Unown), the game must enter promotion if a Bench is present;
    do not leave a permanent empty Active slot or silently pick a substitute.
  * Coin-flip Heads and Tails are distinct branches. A triggered search must
    only happen on Heads, with a legal Bench slot and an eligible card in the
    requested location. Do not substitute an unrelated Pokémon or off-set
    evolved card when none qualifies.

10.14J [LOCKED] C-RELEASE-GATE-017 — PHYSICAL DESTINATION AUDIT (v2.20)
  * Write explicit source and destination for every card in an effect: hand,
    deck, Bench, Prize, attached stack, ordinary discard and ordered Basic
    Energy ledger are not interchangeable. Verify physical UID/card identity
    and quantity before and after each transfer, including return-all cases.
  * 'Up to N' retrieval must allow early Done, handle an exhausted source,
    reject ineligible items and shuffle at the printed checkpoint even if
    the player stops early. DP Drifblim Wind Wave is the regression example.
  * When an attack returns an entire Pokémon and attached cards to the deck,
    include EVERY evolution, attached Tool, Special Energy and Basic Energy.
    For LuckyDex's separate Basic Energy ledger, reconstruct physical Basic
    cards using the CURRENT set's print/art when the printed destination is
    a deck; a discard-ledger transfer would be incorrect. DP Drifloon Blowing
    Wind is the reference regression, including a second-choice opponent.
  * Separately test self-damage, placed damage counters and attack damage:
    counters do not take printed Weakness or Resistance; direct attack damage
    does. Test different Active/Bench targets and choice of target owner.
  * Printed multi-step attackers must preserve the order of selecting targets,
    damage, action costs, card movement, KO, Prize and optional switching.
    A 'no crash' smoke result is never sufficient to un-gate a card.

10.14K [LOCKED] C-RELEASE-GATE-018 — ABILITY / DECKBUILDING / STAGE CLAIMS (v2.20)
  * Continuous Bodies on Benched Pokémon must interact with the actual allied
    Active and respect explicit Body suppression (Magnezone Magnetize). Verify
    the specific payable Energy and actual retreat cost, not just a displayed
    ability description.
  * Test Baby Evolution as an optional activated Power from HAND, with the
    right named partner, healed damage, preserved stack and selected deck
    synergy (Munchlax/Snorlax). An external-era partner must not be invented
    inside a set-only pool just because the Baby has a named target.
  * Every stage release must report exactly how many prints are 1) in the
    catalogue, 2) battle-enabled, and 3) still gated, and list each gated
    identity. At least one actual attack outcome, Power accept/decline,
    player+CPU path, focused/seeded deck and prior-era regression per shared
    mechanic is required before declaring it verified.
  * Do not relabel a staged set 'complete' until 100% of numbered prints and
    all new rule families (especially Pokémon LV.X Active-only level-up,
    previous-level attack/Power inheritance, KO and Prize behaviour) have
    dedicated tests and realistic long-battle/on-device follow-up.

10.15 [CHECK] C-DP1-ERA-001 — PRINTED ERA ISOLATION
  D&P 2007 uses printed ADDITIVE Weakness (+10/+20/+30) and reduction Resistance
  (-20). Apply the printed amount only in applicable era/print; never globally
  replace legacy ×2 calculations. DP Basic Darkness/Metal are true Basic Energy
  (not interchangeable with historical Special Energy prints). A Pokémon LV.X
  levels up ONLY from its named ACTIVE previous-level card and inherits that
  level's attacks/Powers/Bodies; it is not a standalone Basic/Stage 1/Stage 2.
  D&P opening-turn draw/Trainer-Stadium-Supporter restrictions must be scoped to
  applicable games, not backported to legacy EX battles; verify user-approved
  30-card Classic adaptation, implement and test before advertising full DP1.
  Current v0.69.222 STAGE is NOT complete: LV.X, changed opening rules and
  most abilities remain battle-gated. This era-specific rule is a REQUIRED
  future release gate, not proof that all its mechanics have shipped.

  [LOCKED] C-EX11-CHOICE-007: EX Delta Species Vaporeon ex (ex11-110) Evolutionary Swirl triggers on evolution from hand; the owner may decline or shuffle the opponent's hand into the draw deck. If shuffled, the OPPONENT chooses how many cards to draw, from zero to four, subject to its available deck/hand cap. Never force four cards or let the attacker choose the defender's count.
  [LOCKED] C-EX11-META-008: EX Delta Species search Powers count as search, not blind draw; moving existing Energy between Pokémon is Energy transfer, not free Energy acceleration; drawing for the opponent through disruption is not self-draw support. PART 12 — OPEN QUESTIONS / DO NOT SILENTLY DECIDE

  [CHECK-01] Classic Bench and victory format: historical approved design was five Bench spaces and four-point victory with prize values; inspected v0.69.185 engine uses three Bench spaces and five physical Prize cards. Requires user decision before an overall-format redesign.
  [CHECK-02] Decide what happens to unusual official rules involving an explicitly second Defending Pokémon, expanded Bench or physical prize placement in this converted format, card by card. Maintain existing documented adaptations until a specific better rule is agreed.
  [RESOLVED-03 / C-ENERGY-007] Previously open: any effect that explicitly shuffles eligible Basic Energy into the deck must put REAL DRAWABLE cards in the DRAW DECK. No generic diversion back to Energy Zone; see 3.2b. Check unique card-specific exceptions only when their printed text actually uses a different destination.
  [CHECK-04] Any historical Pocket or Classic bug list needs a current repro before it is claimed to remain open; do not infer unresolved bugs purely from old release notes.
  [CHECK-05] Large custom Stadium Bench limits and varied hand/discard/card-picker UX should be checked on real Android touch hardware even where Node assertions pass.

PART 13 — INSTRUCTIONS TO PASTE INTO ANY NEW CHAT
=======================================================================

"We are continuing development of LuckyDex. Read source/docs/LUCKYDEX_MASTER_RULES.txt from the latest uploaded source ZIP FIRST. Treat its [LOCKED] rules as binding and its [CHECK] items as unresolved, not automatically resolved by the code. Review the actual current version and card implementation before editing. Preserve saves, ownership, art, achievements, CPU logic, Test Lab, existing gameplay and all prior expansions. Do not use Codex unless I ask. When I add a new rule or we discover an exception, update this TXT, its changelog, the relevant card/engine tests and any set notes in the SAME patch. For new sets, cover every card, move, metadata, player/CPU deck-builder integration, Special Energy, targeting, cross-set rules and regression tests. Deliver a real, versioned changed-files patch with correct Termux installation instructions. Never mistake 'current code behaviour' for an approved design rule."

  [CURRENT] v0.69.213 enables all 101 EX Dragon Frontiers (ex15) cards after completing the 19 previously gated effects and their outcome-level regression checks. Printed delta identity, choice timing, Prize flips, copied attacks, Energy movements, cross-board counters, pre-damage switching and delayed effects have targeted tests in tests/v07213-ex-dragon-frontiers-completion.test.cjs; the updated ledger is docs/EX_DRAGON_FRONTIERS_v0.69.213_COMPLETION_AUDIT.md.   [LOCKED] C-EX15-OPTIONAL-008: A printed "may" Power or attack effect offers an accept/decline decision BEFORE moving, revealing, discarding or attaching cards; declining leaves all zones unchanged. Multi-step effects must preserve their originally chosen object and cannot silently choose a default recipient. If a recovery effect specifies "1 of your Pokémon", the user/CPU can select an eligible Active or Bench target.
  [LOCKED] C-EX15-DAMAGE-009: A "before doing damage" switch resolves BEFORE Weakness/Resistance, not as post-attack repositioning. Damage modifiers apply in their printed order, especially against Weakness; placing damage counters does not become attack damage and bypasses Weakness/Resistance. Attacks that deal damage to a chosen Bench target must emit one Bench hit event per actual hit.
  [LOCKED] C-EX15-COPY-010: Attack-copy choices must respect the source restrictions, copied attack's original Energy requirements when specified, other printed costs and target/result behaviour; recursive copies must be bounded rather than trapping either player or CPU. Card lookup/search must account for Holon Veil's dynamic delta identity during the live battle. No unsupported generic "special effect" fallback can count as implementing a card.
  [LOCKED] C-EX15-ENERGY-011: A card that discards one Energy and recovers a DIFFERENT Energy cannot immediately recover the same physical card. When discard pools aggregate Basic Energy by type, preserve how many cards of that type existed before the discard to enforce distinct physical identity. Cross-set Holon Energy reprints share their activation rules and legal owner limits.
  [CHECK] The full relevant automated test run is 167/167 (v07205–v07213 selected suites) and does not substitute for Android touch QA, extensive long-match AI decisions, or exhaustively simulating every pairwise card interaction. Four earlier EX Crystal Guardians cards remain gated (ex14-91, ex14-98, ex14-99, ex14-100). No Pocket rules intentionally changed.

v0.69.214 SHARED MECHANICS AND USER-APPROVED UI REFINEMENTS
------------------------------------------------------------
C-PRIZE-CHOICE-001 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Classic Prize awards require the
claiming human player to choose a specific Prize POSITION after a knockout,
not take the next/first card by default. Hidden Prize identities must remain
secret in battle actions, previews, labels and screen-reader text. Cards
previously revealed by an effect must retain face-up identity and artwork.
Each two-Prize ex knockout permits TWO successive choices from the then-
remaining pile. KO/promotion, end-turn and victory resolution must resume only
after all applicable choices. CPU chooses without reading hidden identities.
Pocket point-scoring is unchanged.

C-PRIZE-UI-002 [LOCKED] In a Classic battle, tapping your Prize count opens a
preview of all remaining positions as face-down cards, except those already
revealed by a card effect. After a KO the same choices are actionable. New
reveals (e.g. Island Hermit) must not name an unrevealed Prize in the OPTION
prompt before the choice is committed. A face-up card remains visible until it
is drawn, exchanged or hidden by an explicit printed effect. Preserve the
existing chosen sleeve artwork on hidden positions. Enforce Classic hand cap
after collecting, without deleting cards.

C-DECK-RETREAT-008 [LOCKED] A passive Body such as EX Dragon Frontiers
Swellow delta Extra Wing is Stage 2 Pokémon-ex *retreat support*, not generic
Fire damage synergy. When eligible and Energy-compatible, the automatic
builder should pair its complete Taillow->Swellow line with a COMPLETE Stage 2
Pokémon-ex evolution line and vice versa; never leave an orphan Stage 2 or
invent a third Basic Energy type. Such support is optional for manual decks.

C-CPU-DOZING-009 [LOCKED] Snorlax delta Dozing is an OPTIONAL Poké-Power.
The CPU should use it when active, damaged enough to heal and legal, then
select the healing choice rather than the initial decline option. The
printed Asleep consequence and Bedhead between-turn trigger remain intact;
never turn Dozing into a forced auto-trigger for the human.

C-ENERGY-HAND-017 [LOCKED] Latias ex delta Fellow Boost attaches an actual
Basic Energy CARD FROM HAND and ends the player's turn. Do not consume the
next Basic Energy Zone card directly. The builder should reserve legal
physical-hand Basic retrieval (Mr. Stone's Project where present) so the Power
can function. Its action must not appear without a hand Basic card; inspection
must explain this restriction. Respect once-per-turn and legal target choices.

C-LAB-AUTO-003 [LOCKED] Test Lab Classic auto-builder preferences (selected
sets, attack Energy types, theme, rarity, Basic-only, random/comparison
options) persist independently from normal Classic and CPU opponent settings.
Saving a Test Lab deck preference cannot rewrite owned normal deck data,
rewards, collection, or normal opponent settings. Persist and restore across
Test Lab exit/re-entry, including previous saved schema migration.

C-UI-CARD-004 [LOCKED] In choice menus with recognisable full card artwork,
do not repeat the card name in large duplicate text blocks below the image.
Retain screen-reader alt/ARIA labels, readable prompts, and full text when
there is NO suitable artwork. Display printed Poké-Body versus Poké-Power
identity accurately, rather than labelling every legacy ability a Power.

[CHECK] This v0.69.214 pass changes global Classic KO/Prize timing; extensive
third-party mobile touchscreen and exotic simultaneous KO effects are NOT
fully verified. Historical manual-vs-current Classic board-size conflict
remains unresolved. EX Crystal Guardians' four prior PARTIAL cards remain
gated. See docs/LUCKYDEX_069214_CLASSIC_QOL_AUDIT.md for tests and caveats.

v0.69.215 EX DRAGON FRONTIERS — STAGE 2 EX / DECK-SYNERGY AUDIT
------------------------------------------------------------------
C-DECK-STAGE2-EX-010 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] A deck's automatic selection of a Pokémon whose main Bench Body or Power serves Stage 2 Pokémon-ex must include a COMPLETE, owned, legal Stage 2 Pokémon-ex evolution family when available in the chosen sets. Do not include Xatu δ solely as a weak attacker without a beneficiary or put a Stage 2 ex in a deck without its evolving Basic/Stage 1. Reverse synergy is also important: a Stage 2 ex attacker should favour Xatu δ's attack boost and Swellow δ's retreat reduction when their lines are legal and available. Treat Altaria ex δ's Extra Boost as hand-only Basic Energy acceleration; only include that support with a real way to get physical Basic Energy into hand, not the infinite Basic Energy Zone. The first priority in automatic Energy planning is paying for the Stage 2 ex ATTACKER, not forcing an off-type Bench support attack. Respect user-set type filters, allowed sets, ownership, 4-copy names, Pokemon Star restrictions and 30-card deck size. Special-purpose support can have a nonpayable off-type attack if its passive effect is independently useful, but don't incorrectly claim the off-type Energy is available. This is a general rule for future stages and cross-set engines, not just Xatu.

C-EX15-DAMAGE-012 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Xatu δ Extra Feather supplies +10 attack damage for EACH in-play, unsuppressed allied Xatu δ benefiting a Stage 2 Pokémon-ex, on both player and CPU boards, applied before Weakness/Resistance; do not collapse two separate Bodies into one Boolean bonus. Respect Power/Body suppression and the printed qualifying stage/ex requirement.

C-EX15-META-013 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Attack-effect metadata must identify all inflicted Special Conditions, including combined effects and target aliases such as “that Pokémon,” without falsely treating self-inflicted Sleep as an attack against the opponent. For EX15: Arbok δ has Burn AND Poison; Vibrava δ has Confusion. Use the printed targets for deckbuilding and CPU evaluations, not an overly narrow regex.

C-DECK-CONDITIONAL-011 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] If a featured Pokémon's printed damage, secondary effect or Body uses attached optional Energy of another type, allocate the conditional trigger type to automatic Basic Zone selection where feasible, after mandatory attack types and user-locked filter constraints. EX15 Typhlosion δ's Burning Ball needs Psychic to attack but Fire to reliably enable its printed Burn condition. Do not apply this secondary Energy automatically to unrelated attackers. Pokémon requiring Tools as a condition of an attack or bonus must get owned, legal physical Tools before generic Trainer fillers when possible. A Tool's printed effect must still be independently legal and useful.

C-CPU-EX15-014 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Do not advertise or have CPU attempt Altaria ex δ Extra Boost unless the side ACTUALLY holds at least one physical Basic Energy card AND controls a Stage 2 Pokémon-ex recipient; never pretend the Basic Zone is a physical hand card. Both sides must use identical preconditions and legal choices. Preserve other hand-only Power/Supporter selection constraints.

[CHECK] v0.69.215 code and automated tests cover 101/101 EX15 identities, 83/83 focused legal owned-deck constructions, selected explicit battle state outcomes, all printed EX15 attack action smoke calls with supplied Energy, and seeded CPU deck construction. This does NOT mean every card's effect was independently re-proven in every matchup, nor that long CPU self-play or Android touch UI has been certified. Old EX2/EX3 single-type deck coherence tests fail identically on unmodified v0.69.214 baseline; track as unrelated legacy bugs, not new EX15 regressions. EX14's four PARTIAL cards remain unchanged. See docs/EX_DRAGON_FRONTIERS_v0.69.215_FULL_PASS.md for per-card ledger and precise test scope.


v0.69.216 CLASSIC DECK-BUILDER FOLLOW-UP — CONDITIONAL PARTNERS / CHOICE ART
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------
C-DECK-PARTNER-015 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] A 'preferred partner' in card
metadata is only a recommendation. It MUST NOT introduce an off-Energy
attacking evolution family in a single-type auto-deck unless its own attacks
are payable by the final Energy Zone, or a verified PRINTED in-play dependency
makes it independently useful as a non-attacking Bench support. In particular
EX15 Feraligatr δ Lightning cannot add an unpayable Fighting Meganium δ line
just because the metadata lists Meganium as a preferred partner. In-play
printed Regi-style requirements may intentionally include an off-type Bench
Pokemon, but ordinary editorial partner suggestions are not that exception.
Preserve optional mixed-type support when the player enables it and actual
mandatory multi-type attack costs on the featured attacker.

C-DECK-SUPPORT-016 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Stage-2-ex-only support Bodies/Powers
must have a complete, eligible Stage 2 Pokémon-ex beneficiary family before
auto-selection. Apply the rule at BOTH paths: the support Pokémon as the
selected main/focus or partner, AND a randomly selected earlier Basic which
auto-evolves into the support Pokémon. If the restricted type/set pool has no
matching Stage 2 ex, skip that random core and any inert support partner; an
explicit focus should explain why no viable deck can be built rather than
silently returning a useless Xatu, Swellow or Altaria ex line. A Pokémon Star
such as Charizard ★ δ is BASIC, not a Stage 2 Pokémon-ex, and does not qualify.
Do not violate the user's Energy filter just to activate an optional support.

C-UI-CHOICE-005 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] When a choice tile has working full card
art, show the art WITHOUT a second text-placeholder rectangle. Ensure CSS
[hidden] wins over display:grid/block rules for both fallback and image; if
the image fails to load, hide it and unhide the textual fallback. Maintain
ARIA labels and genuine text-only options. Applies to both player/CPU choice
surfaces using the shared component; no need to duplicate printed card text.

[CHECK] EX15 v216 fixes verified by deterministic Card/CPU builder tests and
static CSS fallback assertions; Android rendering and prolonged matches remain
to be tested on a device. The older EX2/EX3 type/cohesion suites still have
three pre-existing failures (same results as unchanged v214/v215 baseline), not
claimed fixed by this EX15 issue pass. See
LUCKYDEX_069216_CLASSIC_BUILDER_CHOICE_FIXES.md.

v0.69.218 EX POWER KEEPERS STAGE 2 — TARGET CHOICES AND ENERGY REPRINTS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
C-EX16-ENERGY-001 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] EX-era printed Darkness Energy,
Metal Energy and Multi Energy reprints must reuse the matching live engine
rules, not just copy printed Energy text into the card catalogue. Darkness
+10 applies to attacks by Darkness/Dark-name Pokémon before Weakness;
Metal damage reduction applies only when attached to an actual Metal Pokémon
(after Weakness/Resistance); Multi provides one any-type unit unless another
Special Energy is attached, in which case it provides one Colorless. No extra
Energy unit or unrelated Pokémon buff is created. EX16 exact IDs: 87/88/89.

C-EX16-CHOICE-002 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] A choice of an opposing target is
owned by the side named by the printed card, not automatically by the target
owner. Energy Removal 2 (ex16-74): flip first, then on heads let the TRAINER
OWNER choose opposing Active or Bench and subsequently a specific attached
Basic or Special Energy card; the selected card reaches the correct discard
ledger. Mawile ex16-17 Pull Away: when the defending player has 5+ cards,
THE DEFENDER chooses which cards to discard, one at a time until four remain;
next-turn automatic draw follows normal battle timing.

[CHECK] v218 adds 15 newly battle-enabled cards over the staged v217 release,
not a claim that all Power Keepers cards are complete. Retain gating for the
remaining 38 (Stadiums, Fossils, evolution-triggered Powers and Pokémon-ex).
Avoid blindly using Cacturne's Poison Structure unless Sidney's Stadium is
actually in play; the Stadium itself remains gated for decks in this build.
Automated node suites: tests/v07200 through v07218, with the exact final
pass count recorded in EX_POWER_KEEPERS_v0.69.218_STAGE2_AUDIT.md. Android
choice overlays and CPU match duration still need device QA.

v0.69.219 EX POWER KEEPERS STAGE 3 — SHARED STADIUMS AND REAL CHOICES
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
C-EX16-STADIUM-003 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] All five EX Power Keepers
Stadiums are two-sided continuous effects, active only while that Stadium
is in play: Battle Frontier suppresses Poké-Powers AND Poké-Bodies on
evolved Colorless/Darkness/Metal Pokémon; Drake’s Stadium reduces incoming
attack damage to Active Colorless Pokémon by 10 AFTER Weakness/Resistance;
Glacia’s Stadium removes Weakness only from non-ex Water Pokémon; Phoebe’s
Stadium reduces Psychic Pokémon retreat Energy by two Colorless; Sidney’s
Stadium prevents Sleep/Confusion/Paralysis on Darkness Pokémon and clears
those conditions on entering play. Altaria #2’s Synergy Effect heals 10 at
the between-turn checkpoint only while Drake’s Stadium is present and its
Body is not suppressed.

C-EX16-ENERGY-004 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Energy Recycle System lets the
owner choose EITHER exactly 1 discarded Basic Energy to HAND or exactly
3 discarded Basic Energy into the real PHYSICAL draw deck (when available).
Find eligible Basic Energy in both the separate discard ledger AND any
actual card discard; make each recovered type/card a real selection. Do NOT
send the three-card mode to the Energy Zone. Energy Switch must choose an
attached BASIC Energy unit on a source Pokémon and a DIFFERENT recipient;
it must not silently consume/move Special Energy.

C-EX16-SEARCH-005 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Scott searches for up to three
real, eligible Supporter/Stadium cards in any mix, and the player can stop
early. Steven’s Advice offers a genuine numeric draw choice up to the
number of opposing Pokémon in play, capped by the current hand limit; it
is not playable when the owner holds seven or more cards including Steven’s.
Pichu’s Cry for Help searches only Lightning non-ex Pokémon. Baby Evolution
chooses a real matching evolution from hand and removes all damage counters.

[CHECK] EX16 Altaria Surprise currently uses a seeded BLIND random opposing
hand-card selection, which correctly avoids exposing hidden choices but is
not equivalent to user-selectable facedown position; improve the choice UI
before claiming perfect printed-choice fidelity. The automated tests verify
persistent state, not live Android touch selection. Maintain the 24-card
gate and continue through remaining Fossils, Powers and ex/Star triggers.

v0.69.220 EX POWER KEEPERS COMPLETION — PRINTED CHOICES & FOSSILS
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
C-EX16-POWER-006 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] An ability that activates only when
played from hand to evolve (Cradily Evolutionary Call, Walrein ex Chilling
Breath) MUST NOT trigger on deck/effect evolution; Pokémon ★ and Absol ex
Bench-entry abilities similarly require a genuine hand-to-Bench action.
Resolve mandatory and optional choices through owner-correct battle-choice
prompts and the existing CPU/Test Lab mechanism. A KO-triggered Power such
as Lanturn Energy Grounding must run BEFORE the knocked-out Pokémon's
attached Basic Energy reaches the discard ledger, moving a selected eligible
unit directly to its printed recipient without creating fresh Energy.

C-EX16-FOSSIL-007 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Claw Fossil ex16-84, Mysterious
Fossil ex16-85 and Root Fossil ex16-86 act as Colorless Pokémon on Bench
but are still physical Trainer cards; they have no attacks and cannot
retreat or receive Special Conditions. Knockouts discard them but award NO
Prize card and do NOT increment knockout counters. Owner may discard a
Fossil from play on own turn before attacking. Their passive reactions
and evolution lines must keep using real battle slots and source IDs.

C-EX16-DAMAGE-008 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Repeated targeted hits, triggered
Bench counters and reactive attack damage must use the printed order of
choices, prevention and type rules. Armaldo Rock Blast chooses one valid
opposing target per discarded Fighting Energy card; Flygon ex Psychic
Protector discards 0–4 real hand cards chosen by the defender and reduces
incoming damage *after applicable attack calculation*; Absol ex Psychic
Pulse hits only opposing Bench Pokémon already carrying damage counters;
Lanturn Lightning Strike is 50 without discard or 90 after discarding
all Lightning Energy (not 40/90). Printed immunity and optional discards
must remain legal on both player and CPU turns.

[CHECK] v220 is a complete source-level EX16 card-enable pass, 108/108; this
is NOT an exhaustive print-image proof nor Android device test. All 344
selected v072xx automated EX-era/regression checks passed. The pre-existing
Altaria Surprise facedown-selection limitation remains pending. Detailed
card audit and device QA matrix: docs/EX_POWER_KEEPERS_v0.69.220_FULL_PASS.md.

v0.69.221 EX POWER KEEPERS POSTRELEASE AUDIT — PRINT FIDELITY AND CHOICES
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
C-EX16-PRINT-009 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Poison Ring prohibits RETREAT only;
never reuse a shared lock that ALSO disallows attacks. A chosen spread hit
(Rock Blast) is attack damage and must respect the chosen defender's
protection, damage reduction and Stadium modifiers, while observing the
printed exception to Weakness/Resistance. Copy attacks waive only Energy
cost where printed; their other attack-use costs still execute, including
physical deck cards discarded by Volcanic Flame.

C-EX16-OPTIONAL-010 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] 'You may' Bench/evolution Powers
(Absol ex Cursed Eyes, Walrein ex Chilling Breath and Eeveelution Star
Crimson/Yellow/Blue Ray) require an actual Use/Decline choice. A declined
Power MUST change nothing. The CPU must consider each Star Power's impact
on both visible Active Pokémon, and may not peek at the rival's private hand.
Altaria Surprise must offer facedown opponent-hand POSITIONS, NOT card
names/art; the chosen position then returns its card to deck and shuffles.
Sableye Down Draw respects the converted Classic 8-card hand limit.

C-EX16-BUILDER-011 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] For EX Power Keepers-ONLY builds,
explicit single-type filters may not be silently broadened to include a
second required attack type on Flygon ex, Salamence ex or their full lines.
A mixed-type deck needs an explicit compatible selection. Preserve earlier
sets' documented mixed evolution-family conversions as a separate rule;
never break previous Holon/Kingdra/Deoxys expectations merely to change EX16.
Status metadata must include Cacturne Poison Structure (power-originated
Poison) and Kirlia Dream Eater's Sleep payoff. Magneton ex16-16 focus under
a one-set filter remains restricted because a later Magnezone exists outside
that selected pool; do not silently accept an incomplete focused family.

[CHECK] EX16 v221 108/108 enabled; 128 attacks execute in a deterministic
smoke state; 80/81 Pokémon produce legal EX16-only focused auto decks,
with only the documented Magneton focus exception; 64 seeded one-type
builds did not add unselected Energy; nine CPU archetype decks tested.
The selected 357/357 v072xx Node tests passed. These verify a finite
representative matrix, NOT every printed state or Android touchscreen QA.
See docs/EX_POWER_KEEPERS_v0.69.221_FULL_PASS.md for 108-card ledger.

10.14N [LOCKED] C-DP1-TURN-022 / C-DP1-AUDIT-023 — D&P ATTACK DURATION AND ALL-ACTION GATES (v2.23)
  • If printed text says a Pokémon "can't attack during YOUR next turn", the lock MUST target the ATTACKER for nextOwnTurn, not opponentTurn and not only the named attack. Legal-action checks and CPU attack selection must reject every move on that next turn; the lock expires after that turn and is cleared when the Pokémon leaves the Active spot, evolves, or otherwise clears attack effects. Steelix Metal Slash, Onix Tunneling, Rhyperior Rock Wrecker, and Empoleon LV.X Hydro Impact are mandatory regression fixtures.
  • Printed attack protection "until the end of YOUR next turn" is not the same as "during your opponent's next turn". Staraptor Accelerative Dive on heads must persist through both the opponent's turn and the owner's following turn. It prevents damage from attacks by BOTH players, including Brave Heart's self-damage while the guard is active, and expires before the next opponent turn. Tails must do nothing.
  • Full-set verification is not achieved by effect-schema validation alone: each printed attack must be OFFERED by legalActions in a prepared payable/prerequisite-satisfying game state and must EXECUTE from both player and CPU seats with pending decisions resolved to completion. Exercise all Trainers likewise. Test next-turn locks by actually advancing turns, not just checking lock presence immediately after the attack. Test shields by dealing prevented damage, not merely inspecting expiry fields.
  • Preserve catalogue integrity: no printed text placeholders/no-op Trainers; each of the 130 D&P prints (107 Pokémon, 15 Trainers, 8 Basic Energy) has unique printed identity and supported effect. Two-sided attack/Trainer smoke is necessary, but not by itself sufficient for proving every possible printed outcome, random branch, AI strategy or Android animation. Document that distinction honestly.
  • Artwork-choice labels: simple card/Basic Energy art options should not repeat their own name or a generic BASIC ENERGY caption. Visible explanatory labels remain essential whenever outcomes differ, such as copied attacks, hand/Prize operations, voluntary discards, targets and attacks with the same image. Preserve accessible aria-label text even when redundant visible captions are hidden.
  • Keep older set regressions in the same suite; any failure must be classified against the preceding baseline and either fixed or reported, not masked by changing expectations to an incorrect card rule. Run a combined v072xx pass with concurrency limited when prior attempts stall.
=======================================================================

Resistance and damage-prevention order still apply correctly. Stadium replacement/removal must restore ordinary Weakness automatically.

Kind Egg requires Happiny anywhere beneath Blissey, shuffles the selected 0..N cards from hand into the deck, then draws the same count (the shuffled cards may be redrawn).
[LOCKED] Unown M MAGNET must use actual active-only power timing, target Bench choices and coin outcome; its Hidden Power heads hits the defender for 30 and tails causes 30 self-side damage to the chosen own Pokémon instead of damage to the defender.
[LOCKED] Slaking Energetic Impulse is only available while Active and is used once per turn before attacking: heads increases Lazy Blow's BASE damage to 130 for that turn; tails blocks its attacks and retreat for that turn. Its effect expires if Slaking leaves the Active position, even if it returns during the same turn.
[LOCKED] Fossil Excavator is a Supporter with a real choice of qualified Fossil-named Trainer or matching Fossil-evolution card from the actual deck OR discard; never spawn cards from nowhere. Fossil-as-Basic Trainer cards Armor Fossil and Skull Fossil remain gated pending full play, KO and discard lifecycle implementation.

APPENDIX v2.28 — 13 NOVEMBER 2026 — v0.69.235
=======================================================================
[LOCKED] C-REVEAL-HAND-022: When a card instructs a player to LOOK AT an
opponent's hand, show an actual visible card-art hand reveal (not a silent
battle-log array or a deck browser) only after the relevant coin outcome;
respect tails and conditional 'may'. Neo Destiny Unown [T] Tell (neo4-88)
reveals the opponent's HAND on heads, then shares the user's hand to the
opponent; it does not reveal the physical deck. Hand reveal needs an explicit
Continue/Close and must follow the visible coin animation, not obscure it.
This is a general cross-era UI convention. Verify with device QA.

[LOCKED] C-ENERGY-REACTIVE-023: A physical Basic Energy CARD placed onto a
Pokémon by a Poké-Power FROM HAND is a real fromHand energy-attachment event,
including Altaria ex δ Extra Boost and Latias ex δ Fellow Boost. It does
not consume the normal basic-Zone attachment; the event must reach matching
Energy-attached triggers for recipient and opponent. Mysterious Treasures
Electivire LV.X Shocking Tail deals the printed two damage counters for
such an opposing attachment. A generated Basic Energy from the Classic
Energy Zone emits fromZone instead and MUST NOT be mistaken for fromHand.

[CURRENT] C-ALTARIA-UI-024: If Extra Boost is absent, the Pokémon action
sheet explains the real prerequisite: physical Basic Energy in HAND,
Stage 2 Pokémon-ex beneficiary, unused once-per-turn Power, and absence
of Power suppression. Neither Rainbow nor Double Colorless is physical
Basic Energy. This is an explanatory UI change; printed eligibility
was already correct and is preserved for both players.

[CURRENT] Postrelease v0.69.235 source audit: 124/124 battle enabled;
111 Pokémon, 10 Trainers, 3 Energy; 168 attack prints and 37 named
Powers/Bodies; 336 attack-seat path executions, 20 Trainer-seat paths,
24 legal set-only CPU decks across eight types, eight simulated CPU

APPENDIX v2.29 — 13 NOVEMBER 2026 — v0.69.236 AUTOMATED CLASSIC AUDITOR
=======================================================================
[LOCKED] C-AUDIT-001–007: Every new Classic expansion must run the
source/scripts/audit-classic-set.cjs preflight against its current collector
catalog before shipping. The reference rules remain this TXT; the small
machine-readable configuration lives at
source/data/classic-card-audit-contract.json. Update its defaultSetId when
changing the active expansion so GitHub CI audits that expansion automatically.

[LOCKED] Fail a release for definite failures: duplicate/unregistered
prints, mismatched declared count, FULL-vs-PARTIAL inconsistencies, invalid
battle effects, enabled printed placeholders and illegal 30-card CPU decks
for a complete set. Do not enable a card with an incomplete action simply
because its JSON passes the validator.

[LOCKED] Warnings/review flags are distinct from proven faults. Include
unverified stage parents, contradictory Energy metadata, missing custom
handler evidence, no-op passives, printed choices, opponent private data,
Energy zones, repeatable actions, timing and special condition interactions
in a human and deterministic-outcome test follow-up. Explicitly validate
player and CPU choices and source/destination changes. Neither the presence
of a card ID in a test file nor a synthetic attack execution proves
printed-correct effects.

[LOCKED] C-AUDIT-UI-008: Android rendering, ability availability,
coin/prize timing, face-down privacy, readable choice labels and real CPU
strategy cannot be certified by this Node-only preflight. Continue to
playtest on device, investigate displayed errors and maintain real
cross-set outcome regressions.

[CURRENT] The dp2 preflight in v0.69.236 finds 124/124 battle-enabled
prints and zero blocking effect/catalog faults, 16/16 legal generated CPU
decks and 356/356 player/CPU attack/Trainer synthetic action paths
completing; 5 advisory source/semantics review flags still require context.
This is **not** independent proof of all printed cards. Source corrections:
Glalie Craggy Face and Dewgong Cold Fat no longer show misleading gated
Poké-Body descriptions; their existing core damage reduction was already

APPENDIX v2.34 — 14 NOVEMBER 2026 — v0.69.241 PRINT-BY-PRINT AUDIT + CARD-ART CHOICES
====================================================================================
[LOCKED] C-CHOICE-020: Whenever a card effect asks for an identifiable
physical Pokémon, Trainer, Stadium, Tool or Energy card, its choice data
MUST carry the exact `optionCardIds`/`choiceCardId` print identity so
all card-choice overlays render actual card art. Never rely on a label
alone if the physical card reference is available. Cards of the same
species from different expansions must retain the true print. Safe
unique-name fallback is permissible only for zones already known to
the chooser; NEVER expose face-down Prize identities, unknown opponent
hand contents or unrevealed opposing deck cards. Numeric decisions,
pass/fail, yes/no and true hidden options remain text or card backs.
Always distinguish physical Basic Energy prints from Energy Zone tokens.

[LOCKED] C-DP3-CHOICE-021: Furret's Baton Pass transfers ANY NUMBER of
its Basic and Special Energy cards after the optional Bench switch,
including zero, with an explicit Finish/Skip. Nidoking Poison Rub gives
the ATTACKER the option to switch the defending Pokemon BEFORE damage
and Poison. Golem Double Throw selects two DISTINCT enemy Pokemon where
two exist; it cannot strike the same Active twice.

[LOCKED] C-DAMAGE-022: Attacks that deal damage to a CHOSEN Active
Pokemon apply its printed Weakness, Resistance, attack modifiers,
and defensive Bodies/Tools unless explicitly overridden. For Benched
damage, apply the attack's printed exception (usually no Weakness or
Resistance). Distinguish damage COUNTERS from attack damage: counters
usually bypass the standard damage calculation. Verified Secret Wonders
examples: Jumpluff Cottonweed Punch, Cloyster Spine Missile, Golem
Double Throw and Xatu Energy Singe. Knock Outs and Prizes occur AFTER
all selected attack targets/effects have resolved.

[CURRENT] v0.69.241 source tests include fifteen new focused rule/UI
outcome checks in v07241-secret-wonders-independent-audit.test.cjs,
plus full dp3 and previous-set regressions. GitHub preflight runs this
new test file. `docs/SECRET_WONDERS_v0.69.241_PRINT_BY_PRINT_AUDIT.md`
tracks all 132 prints, their registered mechanics and remaining audit
risk tags. `generated/classic-audit-dp3.md` and JSON are reproducible.
These checks do NOT prove every printed ruling or certify a real phone.

APPENDIX v2.35 — 14 NOVEMBER 2026 — v0.69.242 BABY EVOLUTION DECK DEPENDENCIES
================================================================================
[LOCKED] C-DECK-BABY-001 — BABY EVOLUTION PAIRING IN AUTOMATED CLASSIC DECKS
  * Baby Evolution is a voluntary Basic-to-Basic Power from an actual card in hand.
    Its target (Smoochum -> Jynx, Pichu -> Pikachu, etc.) is not encoded by
    evolvesFrom and MUST be modelled as a separate deckbuilding dependency.
  * When auto-building or filling/repairing, never add an orphan Baby Evolution
    print without at least one owned, battle-ready compatible Basic target.
    Selection must obey enabled set filters and the chosen Energy plan.
  * When a Baby Evolution Pokémon is the featured/core card, reserve real
    copies of the target early, before generic support/Trainer filler. For a
    4-copy Baby core, prefer at least two target copies when available.
  * Do not add a third Basic Energy Zone type solely to support a Baby target.
    The target should have at least one attack payable by the chosen zone.
  * A saved draft containing a Baby with no partner can be repaired by adding
    a valid target; if that target is unowned/unavailable, exclude the Baby
    from the generated repair rather than silently preserve a dead combo.
  * Manual decks are not forbidden from including standalone Baby Pokémon:
    this is a recommendation/dependency rule for the auto-builder, not a new
    official gameplay restriction.
  * The shared Classic auto-builder serves both Test Lab and CPU opponents.
    Maintain a versioned regression test for Secret Wonders Smoochum/Jynx,
    older Baby Evolution lines, missing-target pools and Fill/repair.
[CURRENT] Source tests/v07242-classic-baby-evolution-deck-coherence.test.cjs
checks the above; Android Test Lab still needs device verification.
2026-11-14 | 2.34 -> 2.35 | v0.69.242 | Classic auto-builder Baby Evolution
dependency pairing for Psychic Smoochum/Jynx and cross-set equivalents.

=== VERSION 0.69.243 — GREAT ENCOUNTERS STAGED IMPLEMENTATION ===
- Expansion dp4 / Great Encounters has 106 historical card prints: 76 battle-enabled and 30 gated until their full printed actions can be resolved.
- A catalog entry alone does NOT mean it is battle-ready. Gated entries must be excluded from Test Lab auto-build, saved-deck repair and CPU-deck candidates.
- Maintain a numbered ready/gated ledger in docs/GREAT_ENCOUNTERS_v0.69.243_IMPLEMENTATION.md and independent auditor outputs in generated/classic-audit-dp4.{md,json}.
- Test each new mechanic with real game-state assertions: mandatory payments, optional choices, effect sequencing before prizes, hand/deck/discard ownership, bench selection, status conditions, actual damage (including Weakness/Resistance), evolution links and AI decision legality.
- Baby Evolution is not a conventional Stage 1 chain. Include a compatible real partner card (e.g. Igglybuff -> Jigglypuff) in any autogenerated deck.
- Active-only Powers must not be available from the Bench. The shared effect condition selfIsActive now supports this rule.
- If a printed card search/choice refers to a physical card, present a card ID/artwork choice. Textual labels are reserved for decisions, and concealed opponent cards must remain hidden except where the card reveals them.
- An auditor smoke-path skip is not a pass. Synthetic smoke completion also does not prove the entire printed effect or the Android choice UI.
- This release is not a full dp4 certification; its remaining 30 gated prints need a follow-up implementation and separate review.

[LV.X] Garchomp LV.X Restore may Bench a discarded non-LV.X Pokemon as a Basic and attach up to three Basic Energy from discard. Dragon Pulse is an on-Level-Up optional 3-coin Bench effect. Glaceon LV.X Chilly Breath suppresses opposing Powers while Active. Leafeon LV.X Energy Forcing is a separate from-hand attachment; Verdant Dance counts real Energy. Porygon-Z LV.X Mode Crash fires on real Level-Up and Decode reorders physical selected top cards.
[FOSSILS] Dome Fossil, Helix Fossil and Old Amber occupy Pokemon slots at 50 HP and evolve to their intended lines. Attaching eligible Energy from hand can trigger their printed evolution reaction. Manual discard during own turn is not a Knock Out, but an opponent KO awards a Prize.
[HAND ENERGY] Dawn Stadium, Health Energy, Recover Energy and Manaphy Aqua Skin react to actual from-hand attachment; an Energy Zone attachment or a move between Pokemon is not the same event. Call Energy activates with the attached Active Pokemon, searches up to two Basic Pokemon for real Bench slots, and ends the user's turn.
[EEVEE] Espeon Sunlight Veil +20 HP on Eevee evolutions once, and Umbreon Moonlight Veil ignore Weakness / free retreat for that family. Avoid stacking equivalent copies.
[CHOICES] Use actual artwork and physical card IDs for selections, preserve hidden info and allow optional effects to be skipped. Check entry-triggered powers for actual in-play and from-hand timing, and complete effects before Prize selection.
[AUDIT] The 100-card auditor has 0 catalog errors/warnings and 312/312 synthetic smoke actions, 16/16 legal CPU decks; 65 Majestic Dawn and 185 selected older-set regression tests passed. Seven nonblocking manual review flags remain (continuous Powers/Body and reused Energy Search handler). === VERSION 0.69.249 — MAJESTIC DAWN INDEPENDENT AUDIT ===
[FIX] Physical Special Energy attached from hand by a Poké-Power MUST resolve that card's printed attachment triggers. Leafeon LV.X Energy Forcing using Health Energy heals 10 and using Recover Energy removes all Special Conditions, exactly as a normal hand attachment would. Handle these through the shared energyAttached event with the attached physical Energy card ID; prevent duplicated triggers in the ordinary attachSpecial path. Do not trigger either on Energy Zone or movement between Pokémon.
[FIX] Eeveelution Poké-Bodies must recognize the genuine previous-level print on a Pokémon LV.X's stack. A Glaceon LV.X correctly levelled up from Glaceon evolved from Eevee retains Espeon Sunlight Veil's +20 HP and Umbreon Moonlight Veil's no Weakness/free retreat. Do not grant this to an isolated LV.X that lacks an Eevee evolution in its physical stack.

=== VERSION 0.69.250 — FOSSIL EVOLUTION DEPENDENCIES IN CLASSIC AUTOBUILDER ===
[LOCKED] C-DECK-FOSSIL-DEPENDENCY-001 — Automatically generated Classic decks (competitive, random, CPU, Test Lab, and Fill/repair) must NEVER add an evolution Fossil card purely as a generic Trainer/filler Item. The actual deck must include at least one owned, battle-ready, selected-set Pokémon whose evolvesFrom explicitly matches the exact printed Fossil name (Mysterious Fossil, Root Fossil, Claw Fossil, Armor Fossil, Skull Fossil, Old Amber, Dome Fossil, Helix Fossil, etc.). Merely owning a Fossil Pokémon in the collection or having a different Fossil evolution in the deck does not qualify.
[LOCKED] Include Fossils as their evolution-line prerequisites when building a valid complete Fossil line. Related search/support cards (Fossil Egg, Holon Fossil, Fossil Excavator) must not be chosen without an actual compatible Fossil evolution represented in the generated deck. If a user focuses a playable Fossil with an eligible evolution, select that evolution as the core; otherwise give a clear failure rather than silently insert useless filler.
[LOCKED] When auto-filling or repairing a saved deck, drop orphaned Fossils. Do NOT remove Fossils from manual card selection or make otherwise legal manual decks invalid merely because of these strategic autobuild rules. Clefairy Doll, which shares the engine's playFossil implementation but is not an evolution Fossil, is exempt.
[TEST] Use v07250-classic-fossil-deck-dependencies.test.cjs in GitHub preflight, including cross-set/CPU/Basic-only/selected-Fossil-focus cases. A 30/30 legal deck can still be strategically incoherent; validate actual evolution dependencies as well as basic legality.

PLATINUM (pl1) — v0.69.265 BATTLE-ENGINE RULES
- Original 133 numbered and secret collector prints are the authoritative
  source, not placeholder labels. Regenerate with scripts/build-platinum.py
  from data/classic/pl1-print-source.json before producing an APK.
- Distinguish Poké-Powers from Poké-Bodies. Damage Bind disables Powers on
  damaged Pokémon; Time Crystal disables non-SP Bodies only; Power Spray may
  react to the opposing player's Power if at least 3 Pokémon SP are in play.
- A basic Energy Zone type is not the same as a physical Energy card in hand,
  deck, discard, or attached. All choices inspect and move real refs or update
  the appropriate zone ledger. Rainbow Energy supplies ONE flexible Energy.
- Pokémon SP are marked by the authentic printed SP subtype; Energy Gain only
  attaches to SP, and Poké Turn returns the selected SP and ALL attached cards.
- Broken-space Blow and Darkness Lost send a KO Pokémon and its full stack,
  Tools, special and Basic Energy to the owner's Lost Zone. Prize rewards and
  ordinary defeat flow are still resolved normally.
- LV.X is a Level-Up, not a normal evolution; its underlying previous-level
  attacks and Powers must remain available, and its Level-Up prerequisites
  must be retained when the deck builder selects an LV.X.
- Stadium effects such as Galactic HQ / Miasma Valley trigger when a Pokémon
  enters/evolves, not merely when the Stadium is played.
- Every optional attack or Power must present a readable, complete choice
  sequence; expert AI should make legal beneficial choices, not always Skip.
- Fossils should only appear in an autobuilt deck when their printed
  evolution partners are available and chosen; playable Fossils are Basic
  Pokémon while in play but Trainer cards in the deck/hand/discard.
- Run the entire GitHub APK workflow before declaring a build complete. The
  Stormfront deck-builder independent auditor must continue passing.

Check every printed HP, type, stage/line, attack cost/damage/text, Power/Body, Trainer rule and enabled mapping; fail the build on any mismatch. A 133-row catalogue-only count is inadequate.

Vileplume Allergy Flower blocks Trainers but not Supporters; Metal/Dark special energy modifiers and red-armor protections depend on correct Pokemon types.
- Choice-driven attacks and stadiums must select actual physical cards and original legal targets: Rotom swaps Prize/deck, LEGEND Box reveals top ten and benches paired halves with revealed Energy, Flower Shop Lady returns chosen discard Pokemon + Basic Energy, Alph Lithograph THREE returns Stadium to its owner's hand. All hand-attachment effects distinguish hand from Energy Zone or discard. Do not auto choose opponent hand/prizes.
- Current v289 tests are local source validations only until actual GitHub CI APK and phone test. Handoff remains 5 standard Termux lines cd / unzip source / git add / git commit / git push.

BLACK & WHITE: EMERGING POWERS (BW2) — v0.69.298
============================================================
Canonical source: PokemonTCG/pokemon-tcg-data cards/en/bw2.json, pinned as
 data/classic/bw2-print-compact.json and verified by scripts/build-bw2.py.
98 distinct numbered prints: 91 Pokemon, 7 Trainers, including separate
full-art Thundurus #97 and Tornadus #98. All are available in set selection,
collection, Classic deck builder, CPU decks, and Test Lab through the common
registry. Regenerate assets and run tests in the APK build.

Mechanical rules to preserve in future sets:
- Modern Abilities and earlier Poke-Powers/Poke-Bodies are separate classes.
  Gothitelle (Magic Room) prevents only the opposing player's Items, only
  while Gothitelle is Active, unless the Ability itself is suppressed. It
  must not block a Supporter or Item played by Gothitelle's owner.
- Nurturing evolves a chosen owned Pokemon from an actual matching Evolution
  card in the deck, without first moving that card to hand; any ordinary
  evolution-on-turn restrictions are bypassed by this attack's explicit text.
- Helping Hand and Thundurus Charge must consume the selected physical basic
  Energy from the deck or the ordered Energy Zone; never invent attachments.
- Max Potion heals the selected Pokemon and moves *all* its attached Energy
  into the appropriate physical or Basic-Energy discard accounting.
- Great Ball reveals only the deck's current top seven; selecting a Pokemon
  places that physical card into hand and shuffles the rest back into deck.
  'Decline' is a real choice; unavailable non-Pokemon cards are not targets.
- Crushing Hammer, Deleting Glare, and Black Eyes target only Pokemon with
  Energy, and the player chooses which attached Energy is discarded. Black
  Eyes is optional and only usable with Krookodile Active.
- Power Whip, Feint Attack and other chosen-target attacks must present clear
  target choices and honor printed Weakness, Resistance and effect bypasses.
- Optional Superpower and Quick Tail Smash coin choices occur before damage
  matching, with recoil and tails gate applied exactly as printed.
- Shear, Energy Wheel, Hurricane, Tailwind, Charge Beam and Recycle move real
  cards or tracked Basic Energy units between correct battle zones. Special
  Energy is moved as a whole physical attachment, not as a free Basic Energy.
- Phat Sound uses the *same* three flips against every opposing Pokemon;
  active Weakness/Resistance applies but Bench Weakness/Resistance does not.
- Sacred Sword and Zap Cannon restrict only their named attack on the next
  own turn, not all attacks. Encore restricts all except the chosen move.
- Harden checks the final damage threshold (40 or less) after matchup math.
- Include these rules in Classic checks and never assume a schema-clean card
  is functionally correct without an executable scenario test.

CLASSIC DISCARD LEDGER RECOVERY — v0.69.300 (19 Nov 2026)
============================================================
[ROOT CAUSE] Classic Basic Energy detached from Pokemon is recorded as a
  discarded Basic Energy *count* in sides[side].energyDiscard[type].
  Physically discarded cards (including Special Energy and some Basic Energy
  prints) remain in sides[side].discard[]; the separate ordered
  sides[side].energySupply[] is FRESH Energy, NEVER discarded Energy.
[RECOVER] Any source:'discard' + searchChosen effect must offer legal
  physical discard cards and the matching Basic Energy discard ledger,
  both through a validated, visible choice menu. Recovered Basic Energy
  decrements energyDiscard[type] EXACTLY ONCE and yields one real playable
  Basic Energy card in hand. No extra fresh Energy or copies may appear.
[REPRINTS] This fixes ecard3-125 Skyridge Fisherman, hgss1-92 Fisherman,
  dp2-48 Gabite (Gather Up), and pl2-33 Snorlax (Pick and Collect).
  The common resolver also supports future generic discard searches whose
  card filters legally permit Basic Energy; trainer and Pokemon-only searches
  remain confined to physical discard cards.
[CHOICES] Show the type/count of ledger Energy beside physical discard
  card candidates. Stop when the effect's count is satisfied, matching cards
  are exhausted, or the Classic eight-card hand limit is reached. Never
  expose the opponent's discard pool or zero-count entries.
[CPU] Advanced CPU can legally choose from either discard source without
  a stuck effectChoice screen. Preserve the AI's non-skip search preference.
[CI] Include tests/v07300-classic-discard-ledger-recovery.test.cjs in the
  BW2 preBuild gate. The tests cover live Supporter play, both Fishermans,
  Gabite, Snorlax, mixed physical + ledger searches, hand capacity, opponent
  isolation, no-op empty discards, live Energy discard and CPU completion.
