LUCKYDEX 0.69.301 — EMERGING POWERS DISCARD / ENERGY / HAND AUDIT
------------------------------------------------------------------
- Discard effects such as Emerging Powers Recycle use the owner's physical discard
  plus individually counted spent Basic Energy in the Classic Energy discard
  ledger. Recovered ledger Energy is removed from the ledger, instantiated as a
  physical era-appropriate Basic Energy card, and put on top of the deck. It
  must NEVER draw from the fresh ordered Energy Zone.
- A Trainer currently resolving its own effect may be present in the engine's
  discard[] for implementation purposes, but is not a legal candidate to
  retrieve with that same Trainer's effect. Earlier completed Trainers in
  discard are normal candidates. These choices work for human and CPU players.
- Any conditional attached-Energy attack text counts older Rainbow Energy
  prints (including energy encoded as Miracle) as providing the named type.
  Emerging Powers Simisage, Simisear, Simipour and Mandibuzz follow this rule.
- A delayed Trainer search decision, including Great Ball, must not exceed
  the Classic eight-card hand limit. If a choice becomes invalid because the
  hand is full, retain the card in its original deck and shuffle as required.
- Keep exact player choices for Superpower and Quick Tail Smash; losing the
  optional choice is a gameplay regression, even if damage calculation passes.
- Run tests/v07301-emerging-powers-deep-audit.test.cjs at every APK build.

LUCKYDEX 0.69.302 — BLACK & WHITE: NOBLE VICTORIES (BW3)
------------------------------------------------------------------
[CATALOG] Include all 102 distinct collector prints in Noble Victories, including
  97–100 full-art Pokémon, 101 full-art N, and 102 secret Meowth. Never collapse
  duplicate named prints into one attack record. All 153 attacks and 12 Abilities
  must preserve their own printed text and effect definitions.
[RESTORED] Tirtouga (#25) and Archen (#66) have the special printed Restored
  stage, NOT the Basic stage. They cannot be used in opening setup or played
  directly from hand as Basic Pokémon. Cover Fossil (#90) and Plume Fossil
  (#93) look only at the bottom SEVEN *physical* cards of the owner's deck,
  may reveal and Bench the matching Restored Pokémon, then shuffle the
  remaining cards back into the deck. The player may decline. Once in play,
  each Restored Pokémon can evolve normally into Carracosta or Archeops.
[ABILITIES] Black & White Abilities are NOT legacy Poké-Powers or Bodies.
  Leaf Tailor suppresses Weakness only on friendly Pokémon with Energy;
  Carracosta's Solid Rock flips on incoming attack damage and applies the
  50-point reduction AFTER Weakness/Resistance; Conkeldurr's Craftsmanship
  increases maximum HP by 20 per attached Fighting Energy; Hydreigon's Dark
  Aura turns all attached Basic/Special Energy into Darkness, preserving unit
  count; Archeops's Ancient Power blocks hand-played evolutions for BOTH
  players (including Rare Candy), not evolution caused by attacks/Abilities.
[REACTIONS] Jellicent Cursed Body confuses the ATTACKING Pokémon when its
  owner is Active and is damaged by an opponent's attack; Druddigon Rough
  Skin places 2 damage counters on the ATTACKING Pokémon under the same
  conditions. Both trigger before KO resolution, including a lethal attack.
  Rocky Helmet deals 20 damage to the attacker when the equipped Active is
  damaged. Eviolite reduces damage by 20 after matchup only when attached to
  a Basic Pokémon. Do not register these as inert, passive once-only effects.
[DISCARD] Eelektrik Dynamotor retrieves an actual Basic Lightning Energy
  from the physical discard pile OR the separate Basic Energy discard ledger,
  attaches it to an owner's Benched Pokémon, and is used once per turn. Never
  generate fresh Energy or offer a dead CPU action with no legal source/target.
  Volcarona Fiery Dance, Landorus Abundant Harvest and Bisharp Energy Stream
  similarly use an actual discarded Basic Energy card. Super Rod shuffles
  three selected Pokémon/Basic Energy from physical discard and/or the ledger
  back into the physical deck, decrementing the ledger for every recovered
  Basic Energy card. Trubbish Garbage Collection returns a real discard card
  to the deck top; the resolving effect cannot select its own current Item.
[CHOICES] Victini Stored Power moves all attached Energy to one CHOSEN own
  Benched Pokémon. Gigalith Core Cannon selects an opposing Pokémon and
  counts Fighting Energy attached to the attacker (including compatible
  Special Energy). Chandelure Cursed Shadow selects opposing Pokémon to
  receive exactly three damage counters in any legal distribution. N shuffles
  each player's hand back into their own deck, then draws for their own
  remaining Prizes, respecting LuckyDex's eight-card hand cap.
[COINS] Victini Victory Star offers its optional attack coin-flip reroll once
  per turn across copies; V-blast requires BOTH heads to deal damage; V-create
  requires five Pokémon on the owner's Bench for its printed attack; otherwise
  its damage is zero, even if the compact format rarely permits a five-Bench
  state. Never silently relax the printed condition in CPU attacks.
[ANALYSIS] Dynamic damage for Round, Net Force, Electrichain and Core Cannon
  must be computed BEFORE the Weakness/Resistance pass. Electrichain's extra
  damage requires a Lightning Pokémon on the Bench, not its Lightning-type
  attacker alone. Leaf Wallop adds 40 to its own attack on the next turn;
  Slashing Strike cannot use its *named attack* during the next owner turn.
[TEST] Run scripts/build-bw3.py and tests/v07302-noble-victories.test.cjs
  in the preBuild/asset-merge gate. Also run the full classic expansion audit
  with `node scripts/audit-classic-set.cjs --set bw3 --smoke`.

[0.69.307 / Dark Explorers Independent Audit]
* Discard-to-attachment searches MUST filter physical cards to Energy only;
  the separate Basic Energy discard ledger is also a legal source. Searches
  specifically requiring Basic Energy (e.g. Leafeon Energy Assist) exclude
  physical Special Energy; those permitting any Energy (e.g. Charge Beam) may
  attach physical Special Energy with its UID and card identity preserved.
  Never transform a Trainer or Pokemon in the discard into an Energy counter.
* Chosen attack targets MUST enter the ordinary attack damage pipeline.
  Raikou-EX Volt Bolt applies Weakness/Resistance to its Active target,
  ignores those modifiers on the Bench, and discards actual Lightning Energy
  after attack damage resolves. Both full-art and normal prints reuse this.
* Multi-target attacks that can choose the opposing Active (e.g. Kyogre-EX
  Dual Splash) apply normal Weakness/Resistance only to that Active hit;
  Benched hits ignore these modifiers. Preserve explicit choices and do not
  assume that all multi-target damage is Weakness-free.
* Cofagrigus Chuck must determine Tool-discard damage once, prior to type
  matchup; after the attack it discards only explicitly selected physical Tool
  cards and must never apply duplicate direct damage. Unique selection IDs
  prevent duplicate-count exploits.
* Item-play legality checks must honor mandatory costs/targets: Ultra Ball
  cannot be played without TWO other hand cards, Dark Patch needs discarded
  Basic Darkness and a Benched Darkness Pokemon, and Enhanced Hammer needs
  an actual opposing attached Special Energy card. These must not consume a
  card on a knowingly impossible action.
* v0.69.307 build gate: v07307-bw5-independent-audit.test.cjs executes after
  printed source generation plus the v07306 full-set tests. Android build and
  device verification remain separate requirements.


=== v0.69.308: BLACK & WHITE DRAGONS EXALTED (BW6) ===
- All 128 numbered prints are catalogued with pinned official card data; Dragon type is distinct from the Energy types required by printed attacks.
- Blend Energy GRPD (#117) and WLFM (#118) each count as exactly ONE unit, usable as Colorless or one of the four specified types. They never provide all four simultaneously. Special Energy is a physical 30-card-deck card.
- Rebirth is explicitly usable from the physical discard pile once per turn, only with an available Bench slot and three different discarded Basic Energy types. On heads, choose distinct discarded types and attach REAL discarded Energy, preserving the separate Basic Energy discard ledger. Never draw new Energy from the Energy Zone.
- Rayquaza-EX Dragon Burst offers an explicit choice to discard all Basic Fire or all Basic Lightning attached; damage is 60 times physical cards discarded, calculated before Weakness/Resistance. Do not silently select one.
- Ho-Oh-EX / Mew-EX / other Pokémon-EX give TWO selectable Prizes when knocked out; Shedinja Empty Shell awards none. Rescue Scarf returns only the KO Pokémon to hand, discarding its attachments.
- Mew-EX Versatile can use printed attacks of Pokémon in play on both sides, paying costs from Mew's own Energy. Garbotoxin disables Abilities (except Garbotoxin) while its Garbodor has a Tool, including Rebirth, and restores them when the Tool goes.
- Altaria Fight Song gives +20 pre-Weakness to Dragon attacks targeting Active; Bouffer reduces received attack damage by 20; Safeguard prevents attack damage/effects from Pokémon-EX; Giant Cape adds +20 maximum HP.
- Tool Scrapper must allow one or two legal attached Pokémon Tool selections, including both players; second choice may be declined. Physical cards return to the correct owner's discard pile.
- Attack choice, coin and target-resolution log events MUST finish before the Prize picker. Existing Classic 30-card deck/8-card hand/ordered Basic Energy Zone are unchanged.
- APK build gate: scripts/build-bw6.py, tests/v07308-dragons-exalted.test.cjs.

# Dragons Exalted independent gameplay audit — v0.69.309
- Ninetales #19 Hexed Flame counts each distinct Special Condition currently affecting the Defending Pokémon, +50 each, before Weakness and Resistance; Burn is not the sole condition.
- Marowak #61 Vortex Chop adds 30 only if the Defending Pokémon has Resistance; Retreat Cost does not affect the move.
- Probopass #82 Magnetic Lines is optional and may move a REAL attached Special Energy card as well as a Basic Energy counter to the opponent's Bench; maintain its original UID, no minted replacement.
- Registeel-EX #81/#122 Triple Laser damages three distinct opposing Pokémon: the Active target receives Weakness/Resistance normally; Benched Pokémon ignore both.
- Shedinja #48 Empty Shell awards no Prize only while its Ability functions. Garbotoxin or other effective Ability suppression restores the standard one-Prize knockout.
- All five rules above have direct Node regression coverage at tests/v07309-dragons-exalted-independent-audit.test.cjs and are APK build gates.


2026-11-20 / v0.69.310 / BOUNDARIES CROSSED (bw7)
- Register all 153 numbered prints, retaining true printed 216 attacks and 21 Abilities. Printed EX variants retain unique card IDs; EX knockout awards two selectable Prizes.
- ACE SPEC cards are limited to ONE TOTAL card across an entire 30-card physical deck, regardless of different names or prints. Validate in both direct deck editing and CPU autobuild.
- Crystal Edge boosts only White Kyurem-EX, and Crystal Wall sets Black Kyurem-EX maximum HP to 300 only while attached. Removing the Tool restores natural HP without healing existing damage.
- Psychic Musharna Dream Waltz may be used while Asleep and adds 30 damage in that condition. Whimsicott Fluffy Tag boosts the selected new Active only during the next own turn, before Weakness/Resistance.
- Liepard Assist flips a coin and, if heads, lets the attacking player select and copy an attack on one of their Benched Pokemon. Never search for, consume, or auto-play a Supporter.
- Hugh makes the OPPONENT resolve hand size 5 first, and then the player; when over 5 the owning player selects physical cards to move to their DISCARD, not back to deck.
- Audino Busybody is an ACTUAL playable hand action. Reveal and discard Audino, heal 10 on Active, and remove exactly ONE chosen Special Condition (never all simultaneously). The hand action must be reachable in mobile UI.
- Dusknoir Sinister Hand moves real damage counters with explicit target selections. Draw In / Dragon Stream must consume existing discarded Basic Energy from the physical discard or tracked Basic Energy ledger, never create free Energy from the Zone.
- Town Map exposes actual current Prizes, without redrawing or replacing them. Optional attack costs and all Tool attachment effects preserve physical card identity and per-turn limits.
- Apply Weakness/Resistance to Active-target damage and not to Benched damage. One player choice must resolve visibly before a pending Prize selection interrupts the flow.
- Never promote a card to FULL solely because it passes a schema audit. Include direct action and printed-effect regression tests; audit static flags in a separate pass.

  Weakness, Resistance, damage guards and applicable continuous damage modifiers.
  Bench targets ignore Weakness and Resistance. Charizard #20 Split Bomb is
  checked with both an Active Vileplume under Allergy Panic and a Bench target.

  original Special Energy UID moves intact to its owner's physical discard.
  Psyduck #33 Firefighting offers a choice if multiple eligible cards exist.

  Special Energy in its optional 70-damage boost and discards ALL eligible
  Fighting Basic counters and Special Energy cards, preserving their UIDs.
- Flygon #99 Flying Beatdown permits its Grass + Fighting optional discard
  from Basic counters or matching physical Special Energy cards. Two DISTINCT
  units/cards are mandatory; one wildcard cannot fund both costs. Where multiple
  choices exist, the player chooses which Energy to discard.
- Energy Search #128 preserves the distinction between an actual Basic Energy
  card from the 30-card physical deck and a Basic Energy Zone unit. When both
  are available they remain distinct choices; taking an actual card retains its UID.

  selects a real physical deck card into hand and shuffles; this has a

2026-11-20 / v0.69.312 / PLASMA STORM (BW8) INTEGRATION
- Plasma Storm follows Boundaries Crossed (BW7), has 138 collector-numbered English prints (including three secrets), and uses an immutable public PokemonTCG/pokemon-tcg-data source blob SHA 5ae56545ca722ef9ebfee78ae434ce416efa4277. To avoid altered card names/effects, the source must be fetched and Git-blob SHA verified once. The cached compact print file is generated under data/classic/bw8-print-compact.json and committed after `sh scripts/prepare-bw8.sh`.
- All 138 numbered prints must be registered; any attack, Ability or Trainer with unimplemented printed text MUST have battleEnabled=false and deckEligible=false. Never silently substitute noop for printed effects. Printed-effect resolution requires separate Android and manual audits, not just generated-schema coverage.
- Team Plasma is a card-specific tag, NOT a universal suffix on all Pokémon or Trainers. Plasma Energy (#127) is a PHYSICAL Special Energy from deck/hand/discard that provides one Colorless, and cannot be substituted with a basic Colorless Energy Zone counter. Colress Machine selects real Plasma Energy from the physical deck and attaches only to a Team Plasma Pokémon.
- Plasma Energy prerequisite attacks (Moltres-EX Power Flame, Articuno-EX Frost Prison, Zapdos-EX Powervolt, Lugia-EX Plasma Gale) check the attached physical card. Plasma Gale consumes an actual Plasma Energy card with its original UID. Lugia-EX Overflow earns ONE extra selectable Prize only for a KO caused by damage from its attack.
- Team Plasma synergy metadata must distinguish Team Plasma Pokémon, Trainers and Special Energy. Snorlax Teampact counts only Team Plasma Pokémon in play, including itself. Klinklang Plasma Steel prevents damage from Pokémon-EX attacks against the owner's Metal Pokémon; Magnezone Dual Brains permits TWO Supporters that turn, without leaving the extra Supporter unlocked on following turns.
- Hypnotoxic Laser sets Poisoned, and only heads additionally Asleep. Virbank City Gym adds exactly 20 poison damage per between-turn checkup (including stronger poison variants). Crobat Ultra-Toxic Fang uses 40 poison damage as its normal tick, then Stadium bonuses. These effects are NOT extra direct attack hits.
- Lucario Dual Armor gains Metal type with Basic or Metal-providing Special Energy; its deck-building metadata must include a conditional Metal energy preference. Victory Piece lets ONLY Victini-EX use attacks without paying their Energy costs. Eviolite reduces Basic Pokémon attack damage by 20 after Weakness/Resistance, including damage on the Bench if applicable.
- Optional attacks and multi-target effects must show clear choices (e.g. Ludicolo Groovy Dance, Braviary Wild Edge); preserve the selected option across effect-choice continuations. Do not duplicate Active damage when an attack also hits the Bench (Weezing Smogbank / Cubchoo Hail). For Power Flame and other Special Energy checks, use physical attachment identity, not mere Basic Energy counters.
- Trainer items such as Dowsing Machine must pay two actual discard-from-hand costs, exclude the still-resolving card as its own recovery target, and retrieve one selected Trainer from the real discard. Keep source/target selection explicit for Scramble Switch and Escape Rope.
- Bright Veil selective immunity (Togekiss #104) is intentionally gated until an exact per-Item/per-target implementation is available; do not allow a wrong no-op passive. Other missing printed effects discovered by the generator must similarly be gated until fixed.
- Local verification: 70 existing BW6/BW7 regression tests plus nine independent Plasma mechanic tests passed against a temporary registry fixture. Complete 138-card catalogue, CPU autobuild, online first-fetch, Android APK and device validation require the real immutable source to be fetched by prepare-bw8.sh and are NOT claimed as tested yet.
- Always run `sh scripts/prepare-bw8.sh` BEFORE committing/updating this patch; it saves the pinned authentic source, outputs the generated JS and coverage JSON, and runs v07312 tests. GitHub build gates regenerate BW8 first, run previous tests only after it exists, and run both BW8 catalog/CPU and Plasma mechanics tests before packaging assets.

2026-11-20 / v0.69.313 / PLASMA STORM BW8 MASTER-CHECKLIST AUDIT
- After generating the exact pinned 138-print BW8 source, confirm 137 battle-enabled cards, BW8 #104 Togekiss gated, all other cards catalogued with printed attack and Ability identity. A clean schema report is not proof that every attack executes correctly: the static audit currently reports 78 review flags and 270 skipped generic smoke actions (144/416 exercised). These require separate scenario coverage and on-device review.
- Team Plasma Grunt can discard ANOTHER physical Team Plasma Grunt with the same card ID (distinct UID). A cost-payable check MUST exclude only the played card's hand index/UID, NOT all cards with its card ID.
- Dowsing Machine may be played with two other hand cards if one is a Trainer, even when the physical discard currently has no Trainer: the printed two-card discard cost precedes choosing a Trainer from discard. Cannot recover its own still-resolving UID. All selections retain exact source UID.
- Donphan Wreck discards the physical Stadium to the Stadium OWNER's discard, never always the attacking side's discard. Preserve the original Stadium ref and UID.
- Cubchoo Hail and Weezing Smogbank damage the opposing Bench using standard attack damage protections, including attack guards and Eviolite on Basic Pokémon. Their separate Active hit is handled by normal attack resolution, not duplicated in the Bench effect. Classic generic benchDamage now includes Basic Eviolite reduction in the same after-modifiers stage.
- Clefable Moon Guidance must obey normal evolution timing: no evolving a Pokémon played/evolved this turn, no evolving twice this turn; if successful preserve the physical stack, record playedOnSideTurn/evolvedOnTurn and trigger onEvolved events. Target and evolution card are player choices when multiple options exist.
- Scramble Switch's Energy movement is optional: the explicit Done menu option exits without reopening the same chooser. Real moved Basic and Special Energy remain attached to the correct Pokémon without creating phantom copies.
- CPU-deck roles are semantic, not keyword matches. The word 'attached' is NOT Energy acceleration; prior BW8 catalogue tagged 32 Pokémon as ramp despite only six printed ramp effects: Victini-EX #18/#131, Manaphy #34, Vanilluxe #37, Magnezone #47, Blastoise #137. Twenty-six false ramp roles have been removed. Opposing control and status tags must not rely on arbitrary 'discard' / 'poisoned' substrings.
- Verification: 96/96 local independent BW6/BW7/BW8 tests pass across the selected seven suites, including 10 newly added BW8 checklist regressions; 315/315 independently generated BW8-only 30-card CPU decks were legal (9 type choices × 35 seeds). Version v0.69.313 is not yet device-tested or verified through a GitHub APK build; the user previously confirmed v0.69.312 built. Full manual review remains outstanding.

LuckyDex v0.69.319 — BW11 Legendary Treasures checklist extension (2026-11-20)
- Every set has a print-conservation check: BW11=115 numbered +25 Radiant Collection (RC1–RC25); 220 attacks and 19 Abilities from pinned source. Keep RC collector identities distinct from numbered prints and preserve exact image/card IDs.

LuckyDex v0.69.321 — XY Base first-pass checklist (2026-11-20)
- XY Base xy1 has exactly 146 collector prints, including 120 Pokemon, 15 Trainers, 11 Energy, 202 printed attacks, 12 printed Abilities, Mega Venusaur-EX #2 and Mega Blastoise-EX #30. Source is pinned to PokemonTCG/pokemon-tcg-data Git blob 0f709a58e008e0de36e02bc11f7a070607e17b31. Generate the full card catalogue before APK asset merge; local stub is not a complete set.

Basic Fairy Energy comes from the Energy Zone, while printed Rainbow Energy and Double Colorless are actual physical UID-bearing cards with distinct attachment rules. Rainbow attachment from hand inflicts 10 damage, and Double Colorless provides two Colorless units, not two physical cards.

Hard Charm and Furfrou Fur Coat reduce opposing attack damage to the holder including on the Bench; Muscle Band adds 20 before Weakness only when attacking the opposing Active. Sweet Veil prevents and clears conditions if the holder has Fairy-providing Energy, unless suppressed.
- XY printed selectable effects must preserve each unique physical UID, distinguish special attached Energy from the discarded Basic Energy ledger, and support optional Done, legal choices, hand cap and KO/prize timing. Great Ball searches the actual top seven cards only. [SUPERSEDED v0.69.332] The old restriction requiring Professor's Letter to take only PHYSICAL deck Basic Energy was inconsistent with global C-ENERGY-002 and is no longer valid: Professor's Letter may find eligible fresh Basic Energy in the ordered Energy Zone (or real Basic cards shuffled into the draw deck); selected Energy goes to HAND, not directly onto a Pokémon. Energy assignment needs typed recipient choice.
- CPU decks must obey a true 30-card composition, four-copy limits, complete evolution dependencies, unique Special Energy identities, Fairy typed attack cost, and no gated prints. The XY-only sparse emergency recovery cannot affect older sets, user-built decks, or explicit focus.
- Entire prints with one missing attack/Ability/Trainer rule are gated and documented; no optimistic fake no-op. In particular revisit two-sided Rapid Spin, Stance Change, Fossil/stack return, Geomancy choice, Evosoda evolution timing and exact Tool/KO reactions in the independent set audit. Printed-card conservation is NOT equivalent to battle-readiness.
- Local mapping schema validation: 127/127. Synthetic 146-print fixture (not shipped): 13/13 XY checks pass. Source-dependent independent tests skip locally and must execute in GitHub AFTER pinned XY generation. Do not claim GitHub build success or final battle-enabled count until verified there. Phone installation remains unzip -> git add -> git commit -> git push with no preparation command.

LuckyDex v0.69.322 — XY Base independent master checklist / GitHub build repair (2026-11-20)
- The v0.69.321 GitHub APK attempt failed BEFORE XY generation because the cross-release Supreme Victors test found Gradle 0.69.321 while boot JS and both UI version labels still said 0.69.320. All three version strings and incremented versionCode must agree on every release. Do NOT bypass earlier-set build gates to make a new set build.
- XY Gogoat Charge Dash and Rhyhorn Dig Out were unintentionally battle-enabled without any runnable handler, which could throw at attack resolution. Charge Dash's optional +20 / 20 recoil needs explicit user choice; Dig Out consumes EXACTLY one physical deck card and only attaches a qualifying Fighting Energy card, otherwise sends it to the real discard.

Vivillon Conversion Powder chooses ONLY Asleep or Poisoned and routes through normal status immunity. Magcargo Magma Mantle offers keep/discard-top before damage and adds +50 only when a Fire Energy card is actually discarded. - XY Conkeldurr Wake-Up Slap checks status BEFORE damage, adds 60, and removes conditions AFTER damage. XY Skarmory-EX Joust removes ALL opponent Active Tools BEFORE resolving damage and preserves the discarded Tool UIDs. Charge Dash and Electron Crush optional bonus/discard choices must never consume the first eligible Energy without player consent.
- XY Gourgeist Spirit Scream places counters on both Active Pokemon until each has 10 remaining HP, never reduces existing counters. Eerie Voice places two counters on each opposing Pokemon (active and benched), subject to attack-effect protection. Mine reveals the top of the opposing deck only to the player who used it, who may then choose to shuffle that deck. Mad Mountain mills only after both required heads and counts existing attacker damage counters.
- XY Energy Salon searches up to three DIFFERENT Basic Energy types: choose physical Basic Energy UIDs from the real draw deck or eligible Energy Zone types, consuming each selected supply unit; no duplicate type. Pickup recovers two chosen Item references and recognizes XY-era Tools as Items. Recover discards a selected attached Energy to heal the attacker fully. Super Potion targets a damaged Pokemon with attached Energy, discards an EXACT selected unit/physical reference, and heals 60 only when cost is paid.
- XY Geomancy chooses two DIFFERENT Benched Pokemon, each receiving actual Fairy Energy either from a physical Basic card in the draw deck or an available Fairy entry in the Energy Zone; shuffle once after the complete search. Emolga-EX Energy Glide attaches actual Lightning Energy from physical deck or eligible Zone before allowing the required Bench switch. Flame Charge similarly searches Fire Energy from either source. Yveltal Oblivion Wing recovers ONLY actual Basic Darkness discard ledger or physical discard, and Yveltal-EX Y Cyclone moves exact attached Energy references.
- All collector prints remain catalogued, but only battle-enabled prints may enter the CPU/manual builder. The explicit source-level XY gating list after v0.69.322 contains 19 complex prints (4, 11, 29, 32, 35, 44, 48, 50, 54, 71, 74, 76, 85, 86, 89, 103, 115, 116, 142). Other unsupported printed text may add generated gates; NEVER claim 127 battle-ready or any final enabled count without running official pinned catalogue generation.

Local synthetic fixtures (clearly not official cards) are for schema/CPU smoke only and are explicitly REMOVED before packaging. GitHub `auditXY1ForApk` must execute BOTH v07321 and v07322 tests after pinned generation. No bypass of audit or APK build gates. Previous-set regressions must remain green.

XY FLASHFIRE (v0.69.325) — SET INTEGRATION NOTES
------------------------------------------------
Flashfire has 110 printed records: 109 numbered plus Blacksmith 88a.
The GitHub Gradle build generates the authentic, pinned source before testing and
asset merge. Never ship/mock generated card data or claim the APK built locally.
Fairy Basic Energy, Pokémon-EX and Mega Evolution are shared rules.
Pokemon attacks that search for Basic Energy from the deck may consume the
ordered Classic Basic Energy Zone when Basic Energy prints are not physically
in the deck; they must never invent physical Special Energy, discard recovery
or hand attachments. Blacksmith only attaches actual discarded Basic Fire
Energy (physical cards or documented discard ledger), and Fiery Torch must
pay its actual Fire Energy card from hand. Mega evolution from hand ends turn.
CPU rescue must remain restricted to the requested set, avoid orphan evolution
lines and require actual owned 30-card legal decklists. Keep unimplemented
printed moves, abilities and Item actions visibly gated. Audit each print and
rarity variant independently, and preserve alternate collector IDs like 88a.
The v0.69.325 integration does not replace the prior XY Base master audit.

XY FLASHFIRE v0.69.326 AUDIT: Caterpie/Metapod Adaptive Evolution applies on
first turn or turn played only while the Ability is unsuppressed, without
bypassing non-timing restrictions. Floette Flower Veil adds 20 maximum HP per
unsuppressed Floette to allied Grass Pokemon, including Benched. Dragalge
Poison Barrier forbids retreat of the opposing Poisoned Active (and does not
remove Energy or prevent switching by attacks). Protection Cube prevents
recoil from the holder's own attacks, never opponent damage. Snorlax Stir and
Snooze requires two heads to wake at between-turns checkups. Durant Chip Off
uses exact random physical hand refs down to four cards. Forretress Thorn
Tempest triggers only on evolution from hand, offers a genuine optional choice,
and when chosen hits all opposing Pokemon with one counter each before KO
resolution. Maractus Exciting Shake changes the
next owner's Prickly Needles to six coin flips, not the current attack.
Keep unresolved cards gated and test card/UID conservation and 30-card decks.


XY FLASHFIRE v0.69.327 — 19-PRINT COMPLETION / ADDITIONAL SHARED MECHANICS
- Flashfire 110 distinct collector rows remain immutable, including Blacksmith 88a and the Mega/full-art prints. All 19 formerly gated print IDs (7,9,20,21,23,27,29,34,35,38,39,40,43,49,50,74,82,85,101) now have explicitly mapped effects; still require official GitHub-generated 110/110 coverage verification before claiming final production battle-readiness.
- Leaf Draw discards an explicitly selected physical Basic Grass Energy from hand before drawing three; one use each owner turn. Whiplash repeatedly flips a coin and lets the attacker discard an explicitly selected opposing attached Basic/Special Energy for each heads until tails, preserving each physical card identity.
- Scorching Fang offers base attack or optional Fire-providing attached Energy discard; only a paid discard grants +30. Rock Rush offers the exact subset of hand-held Basic Fighting Energy to discard BEFORE dealing 30 damage per card. Do not choose hand cards for the player automatically, infer discarded cards from the Energy Zone, or silently consume any Special Energy.
- Counterattack Quills retaliates for 20 damage counters only when an opposing attack damages the Active Qwilfish, even on lethal attack damage, not from burn/poison or non-attack sources. Heart Wink only prevents the NEXT opponent turn's initial draw on heads, never Trainer/attack draws.
- Energy Grace is optional Milotic self-KO to attach exactly three discarded Basic Energy units to ONE selected allied NON-EX Pokémon; choose a legal recipient and then each physical discarded Energy/ledger unit. A ledger type count of 2 means TWO distinct Energy units, not one option. Finish all attachments before KO/Prize selection, then award opponent the proper Prize and promote as normal. Never allow a Pokémon-EX recipient.
- Rescue selects and shuffles three actual discarded Pokémon cards to the deck, retaining physical UIDs. Fang Snipe reveals the opponent's real hand and allows the attacking player to select one Trainer to discard. Revival benches a selected Basic Pokémon from the OPPONENT's discard, enforcing their Bench limit. Cursed Drop places four individually targeted damage counters on opposing Pokémon.
- Shadow Void repeatably moves one counter from another of your Pokémon onto Dusknoir (not from Dusknoir to itself); Pain Pellets chooses an opposing Pokémon and places damage counters equal to its current damage counters. Ear Influence lets the player distribute existing opposing damage counters between DIFFERENT opposing Pokémon, never create new counters.
- Flash Claw must request a real HAND-card selection from the DEFENDING player, not the attacker. Goodra Gooey Regeneration repeatedly discards selected attached Basic or physical Special Energy to heal 60; it is not once per turn. Furret Buried Treasure Hunt takes two explicitly selected physical cards from the top four of deck to hand and discards the other two. Lopunny Big Jump returns the entire evolution stack, Tools, physically attached Special Energy and Basic Energy to hand, respecting the app's hand cap and preserving UIDs as engine rules permit.
- Magnezone-EX dual 50-damage attack uses native two-distinct-Pokémon target choices; do not automatically redirect to the Active. Both ordinary and full-art Magnezone-EX use the identical effect. The opponent's Basic protections, HP and the two separate targets still apply. Basic-only separate targeting must not be conflated with bench-only splash damage.
- CPU ability legality: do not offer Energy Grace without a non-EX recipient and three real discarded Basic Energy units, Leaf Draw without a physical Grass card in hand, Shadow Void without allied counters, or Gooey Regeneration with no damage/attached Energy. Once-per-turn rules must be enforced by the shared battle engine. Complete evolution lines, proper type supply, full print ownership and 30-card compositions continue to apply.
- Add v07327 Flashfire completion tests to auditXY2ForApk AFTER pinned official source generation. Unit tests for each effect AND real core-action tests for Energy Grace Prize/promotion order and Flash Claw opponent selection must pass; never replace the genuine build with a synthetic source fixture.


CLASSIC BATTLE + BUILD v0.69.328 — USER-REPORTED REGRESSIONS
- All attack costs must normalise legacy array-form Energy symbols before displaying, labelling, or testing payment. [C,C] means CC, not C,C; [R,R,C] means RRC, not R,R,C. Commas are NEVER Energy symbols. Every attached Energy of any type can pay a Colourless unit, after satisfying required typed symbols. Do not silently rewrite canonical printed attack costs.
- Reshiram BW1 26/113 and BW4 21: Outrage CC 20+ (10 extra per own damage counter), Blue Flare RRC 120 (discard two attached Fire Energy). Two attached Fire Energy fully pay Outrage; three Fire or two Fire + any other attached Energy pay Blue Flare.
- Noble Victories Vanilluxe 29 Double Freeze uses shared coinDamageStatus: EXACTLY two logged visual flips, 40 damage per heads (0/40/80), and Paralysis if at least one heads. Use effects that log coin events so the battle UI can animate the result before prizes.
- Flashfire Sneasel 50 has Scratch FIRST (index 0) and Flash Claw SECOND (index 1). Flash Claw must ask the DEFENDING player which of their own physical hand cards to discard. Audits must choose attacks by printed name, not assume index zero.
- Register the XY era explicitly in both player and CPU Classic set selectors. Both xy1 and xy2 are generated BEFORE Android asset merging, and builds MUST fail if an empty catalogue stub would reach the APK (146 XY Base + 110 Flashfire prints required).
- Keep the working GitHub pinned Git-blob XY catalogue generation and SHA-1 checks; build 617 reached the generated XY2 audit, so its failure was a test assertion rather than a download failure. Do not bypass a failed audit or conceal missing source.

CLASSIC BATTLE v0.69.329 — GLOBAL PRINTED ATTACK ENERGY CONTRACT
- All Classic attack cost data MUST be compact typed Energy symbols (G R W L P F D M Y C), never comma-separated or an array at consumer boundaries. Normalize once at registration, reject unknown symbols; preserve official printed types and counts.
- One symbol consumes one provided Energy unit. Any typed Basic Energy pays a Colorless (C) requirement; pure Colorless Energy cannot pay printed typed requirements. Double Colorless contributes two C units. Compatible special/wild Energy may substitute named types only according to their attachment conditions; never double-spend a single physical unit.
- Battle action legality, attached Energy explanations, UI icons, AI energy valuation and deck builder all use the same canonical cost. Fairy Energy (Y) is pink Fairy, not Colorless; Fairy Basic Energy and its actual icon are supported.
- Audit each future deck across every attack cost, both typed and Colorless payment, exactly matching energy icons, runtime menu and available pinned-source print records. Run `node --test tests/v07329-classic-global-energy-audit.test.cjs` and keep the APK preBuild audit enabled.
- Full-Classic offline audit at v0.69.329: 9,725 moves across 59 set IDs; 508 previously array-shaped costs in Plasma Storm, Plasma Freeze and Plasma Blast normalized. 15 locally bundled source-print sets showed zero printed cost mismatches. XY must be generated before the final Gradle audit.

2026-11-21 / v0.69.333 / CLASSIC ALL-ERA DECK QUALITY AUDIT
=======================================================================
[LOCKED] CROSS-ERA AUTOBUILD CONTRACT (not only newly added sets)
  - Decks must contain exactly 30 real, owned, battle-ready cards and legally coherent evolution families, with sufficient Energy Zone types for intended attackers. A genuine Bench-only support Pokémon should not force dead third/fourth Energy types just because its unused attack is off-type.
  - Auto-selection of Trainer fillers should consider marginal role synergy. Prefer a spread of real usable draw, Pokémon search, evolution support, recycling, retreat/healing, and appropriate counterplay instead of defaulting to four copies of the top few generic Trainers. Four copies are NOT intrinsically illegal or banned, especially when a single-set print pool has only one or two useful Trainer cards; retain any user-selected legal four-copy package.
  - Printed target conditions must be respected: Giovanni/Brock/etc Trainers only with the corresponding named Pokémon, Rare Candy with relevant Stage 2 targets, Apricorn Maker only if a Ball card is included, Heavy Ball only if a compatible target exists, LV.X/SP support only with actual targets, and other role dependencies validated from printed text or existing executable effect metadata. Don't allow meaningless Stadium/Tool filler to crowd out the core line.
  - Recognise Trainers implemented via legacy neoRule/printed text, not only generic effect handlers; this information must feed both ranking and post-build synergy reporting.
  - ACE SPEC total limited to one; Miracle Energy limited to one per deck; no generic special Energy filler without a payoff. Basic Energy continues to come from the hybrid Energy Zone, not physical deck padding.
  - Prefer an initial 2-copy ceiling for AUTOMATIC generic Trainers, with an adaptive 3/4-copy fallback only if inventory cannot complete a coherent 30-card deck. Early sets with extremely small Trainer inventories may exceed the usual 18 Pokémon soft cap to complete a legal deck. Manually retained copies are exempt from soft caps.
  - Permanent test: `node --test tests/v07333-classic-deck-quality.test.cjs`, enabled in GitHub preBuild after the entire attack-energy audit. Current locally bundled Classic catalogue: 59 fully populated set IDs with two styles each = 118, plus six focused Trainer builds, ten mixed-era builds, eight CPU samples and two retention/support-specific builds, no invalid/missing Energy, dead-effect Trainer, ownership or 30-card failures. The three build-time generated BW11, XY1 and XY2 source catalogues were empty in local archive and are instead gated by their separate upstream generator-specific audits. Never represent this as proof of an installed APK or real CPU match quality.
  - Keep full player/autobuilder and CPU deck selection logic in step: audit opponent deck generation too when changing shared candidate/ranking logic. Don't use any unrequested Codex tools.

2026-11-21 / v0.69.335 / XY FURIOUS FISTS — EFFECTS, SPECIAL ENERGY, FOSSILS & AUDIT
=======================================================================
[LOCKED] RULES THAT APPLY TO FUTURE EXPANSIONS
  - Implement printed effects at their actual source/target/timing. A build passing is not proof of correct gameplay. Never silently assign no-op, substitute a random effect, or label an unsupported print FULL.
  - Strong-style conditional Special Energy: enforce printed recipient type both when attaching AND after a type-changing evolution. Damage modifiers apply only to the eligible holder and correct attack target; Herbal-style on-attach healing must obey healing prevention.
  - Stacking Fighting boosts must resolve before Weakness/Resistance: Fighting Stadium applies only to opposing active Pokemon-EX and both sides; Strong Energy only to Fighting holder; Machamp Fighting Fury is an independent team aura. Bench damage cannot receive Active-only bonuses.
  - Never use a Restored Fossil as an ordinary Basic Pokemon. Fossil Researcher uses chosen copies of its named species from the physical deck, while Jaw Fossil/Sail Fossil examine its physical bottom seven. The autobuilder must preserve Fossil/Evolution dependencies and no empty Fossil packages.
  - A Tool that changes KO survival must be checked after damage but before KO/Prize resolution, and physically removed after activation. A Tool preventing statuses must also remove existing statuses on attachment when printed.
  - All chance effects must log visible outcomes. Noivern Echolocation and Accelgor Afterimage Strike require a real defensive coin roll; pending Coin decisions must finish before selecting prizes.
  - Physical Trainer/Pokemon search, actual discarded Energy, and fresh Basic Energy Zone searches are distinct. Vibrava Charge Energy may use the existing Basic Energy Zone search contract; Korrina, Fossil Researcher, Jaw/Sail Fossils, and Tool Retriever must use actual card UIDs.
  - Forcing opponent Supporters with attacker-owned choices is NOT equivalent to playing your own Supporter. Keep Hypno #36 Hand Control marked PARTIAL/deck-ineligible until this cross-player interaction exists. Patrat #84 Safety Check IS playable with a private reveal in the choosing player’s option pane and no public card-identity log. Build-time print coverage must report any additional unsupported text.
  - Keep player and CPU deck-building selection, legality and Trainer synergy audits running after each new set; do not regress the v0.69.333 cross-era builder improvements.

2026-11-21 / v0.69.336 / XY3 FULL CARD-BY-CARD AUDIT + HYPNO
- Always test every printed attack and Ability against the pinned official source, including names, ordered costs, mechanics and alternate art. Every unhandled nonempty effect must gate the corresponding print out of battle and autobuild; no fake FULL status or silent no-op.
- Hypno Hand Control: opponent reveals hand; attacking player selects whether and which opposing Supporter to play; the physically removed Supporter goes to opponent's discard. Resolve its effect against the original owning player's hand, deck, discard and board, but all choices belong to the attacking player (including nested searches). The attack still ends the attacking player's turn. Both CPU and player direction must work.
- Raid conditional damage requires an evolution this very turn. Thorough Crunch flips two visible coins, then the attacker selects a real Energy on the opposing Active for each heads, removing physical cards or recording basic Energy discard.
- Permanent v07336 independent audit runs on generated 114-print xy3 before APK merge: 159 attacks, 14 Ability occurrences, 18 Trainer rows, evolution and energy symbols, alternative art equivalence, executable effect schemas, and multiple 30-card builds including CPU-compatible shared builders.
- Bump **both** index.html build IDs (`titleBuildVersion` and `appBuildVersion`) when incrementing Gradle and app JS version; the Supreme Victors test is a global release invariant and MUST be run before every upload.

checks passed. The remaining test compared Heracross-EX #4 against full-art
#105 and found the general Pokémon-EX Prize rule misplaced in Giga Power
#4's ATTACK text, while #105 left the rule at Pokémon level.

strips only the exact duplicated generic Prize suffix when that Pokémon
already contains the identical Pokémon-EX rule in card.rules. Both the

behaviour and Giga Power's proper gameplay effect are preserved.

=== v0.69.339: XY Phantom Forces (xy4) initial integration ===
Canonical official set source: PokemonTCG/pokemon-tcg-data `cards/en/xy4.json`,
immutable Git blob `85ab15304b4741c1f5601ee786fc8f3a764326f9`.
All 124 printed records must survive import: numbered 1-122 and alt prints 24a
(M Manectric-EX) and 65a (Aegislash-EX). 162 attacks, 15 Abilities.
The build uses scripts/build-xy4.py BEFORE asset packaging and any XY4
catalogue tests; the local empty placeholder is never acceptable in an APK.
Keep cards with unimplemented mandatory effects visible for collection, but
`battleEnabled=false` and `deckEligible=false`, including CPU autobuilds.
Night March must count physical discarded Pokemon that actually have the
printed Night March attack, from the attacking player's own discard pile.
This excludes Energy ledger, unused Energy Zone, and opponent discard.
Dimension Valley removes precisely one Colorless attack-cost symbol from
Psychic Pokemon on both sides (no reduction of printed Psychic requirements).
Mystery Energy only attaches to Psychic Pokemon, provides one Psychic, and
reduces its holder's retreat cost by two per attached copy; off-type evolution
must discard it. Double Colorless Energy contributes TWO Colorless units.
Unfinished Team Flare Gear, Spirit Links, Robo Substitute, abilities and
specific printed effects remain gated, not mapped to fake no-op effects.
The XY4 audit must verify version parity, generated collector print count,
energy costs, effects, player and opponent set selectors, and legal 30-card
single-set decks. Use the shared deck-quality logic, do not bypass it.

=== v0.69.340: XY4 Phantom Forces GitHub Energy-cost audit fix ===
Classic attack costs are canonical *symbol strings* in the shared registry,
e.g. GC, FFCC and CC, even when the immutable official XY4 print data uses
symbol arrays, e.g. ["G","C"]. When independently auditing printed costs,
normalize both structures to identical ordered symbol strings, preserving
symbol count and type. Never loosen a printed-energy comparison to mere
convertedEnergyCost or total count. Apply this comparison to all 124 XY4
prints after GitHub generates the official catalogue; keep the exact print,
spell/attack and deck gating tests enabled. This repairs the stage-1 CI-only
false-negative (five of six prior XY4 tests passed). The repair is audit-only:
no Pokémon abilities, attack rules, costs, or deck-building decisions change.
Version parity is mandatory across Android Gradle, HTML title and app boot JS.

VERSION-CHECK TEST POLICY (introduced v0.69.340):
Any persistent set-audit test checking app build version MUST read versionName
from app/build.gradle, require Android versionCode to match the release, and
compare the same value to BOTH HTML build markers and the JavaScript boot
marker. Do not hard-code a particular released version in an old set's
regression test; otherwise all later releases fail CI when version changes.

=== v0.69.342: XY Phantom Forces full-completion audit / 124 official prints ===
- The pinned official `xy4` catalogue has exactly 124 records, 162 printed attacks, and 15 Ability print occurrences, including alt prints 24a and 65a. Do not claim full set completion if GitHub's generated-catalogue audit fails or if any card remains gated.
- All attacks must use their precise printed Energy symbols (Classic may store cost as a compact string), and all printed mandatory effects must be actual, target-aware mechanics, not a silent noop.
- Venomoth Dizzying Wind: each Trainer played by the opponent on the next turn gets a separate coin check; tails prevents the Trainer effect but consumes/discards the card and appropriate once-per-turn usage.
- Pachirisu Trick Sticker: temporarily override only the Defending Pokemon's Weakness to Lightning through the end of the user's next turn, without changing the weakness multiplier. End when the target leaves the Active position.
- Heliolisk Parabolic Spark: the attacker chooses how many eligible Lightning Energy cards to discard before attack damage is decided (zero is allowed); each genuine physical/attached Energy discard increases damage by 30; the choices resolve before Prize handling.
- Head Ringer and Jamming Net are Team Flare Tools on the *opponent's* Pokemon-EX without an existing Tool. Head Ringer increases that Pokemon's attack costs by one Colorless; Jamming Net reduces its attack damage to defending Pokemon by 20 before Weakness/Resistance. Discarded opponent-attached Tools return to the actual card owner's discard. CPU follows the same legal choices.
- Robo Substitute is an Item in hand and deck, but once played becomes a 30 HP Colorless Basic-like Bench Pokemon. It cannot Retreat, can be discarded voluntarily by its owner before the attack, counts as a Knock Out when KO'd but never grants a Prize. It is not eligible as an opening Basic.
- Jellicent Ensnaring Spray adds 10 damage per Energy card genuinely attached to the opponent's Active Pokemon (including attached Special Energy); Galvantula Cobweb Trip chooses the opponent Bench Pokemon to switch into Active, then Confuses the newly Active Pokemon.
- All Phantom Forces alternate prints must preserve their real attack costs, attacks and Ability names; legitimate source-text differences in decorative rule wording are not gameplay differences.
- Permanent tests/v07342-phantom-forces-final-audit.test.cjs audits actual battle-state transitions locally and requires all 124 generated official prints and legal 30-card decks on GitHub. The Gradle auditXY4ForApk task MUST depend on generation and run this audit before Android assets merge.
- Keep Gradle versionCode, versionName, HTML build banners and JS boot marker synchronized on every patch.

=== v0.69.344: XY5 Primal Clash stage 2 — SHARED Ancient Traits, Spirit Links and Special Energies ===
- Source baseline: user-confirmed successful v0.69.343 APK build. Do not claim v0.69.344 GitHub APK success before CI verifies it.
- Ω Barrage: second attack immediately following first if source Active persists, including after KO/promotion; no ordinary actions between attacks, end-turn triggers and status check only after final attack. CPU uses same legal attack rules.
- α Growth: two physical hand Energy attachments maximum to SAME trait holder this turn; Energy Zone and Trainer/attack/Ability attachments do not consume the special hand-attachment bonus. A full exact simultaneous-attachment UX should be checked on device.
- α Recovery: heal double actual reduction in damage, gated by no-healing effects, tracked by physical slot UID. Test multiple healing sources and effect-choice resumes.
- Ω Barrier and unimplemented Primal signature attacks remain GATED; never silently treat their printed effects as no-op.
- Matching Spirit Links (Aggron, Gardevoir, Groudon, Kyogre) prevent only the matching Mega/Primal Evolution's turn ending; a Spirit Link is a regular Tool attachment and auto deck builder adds it only if a matching evolution is in the deck.
- Shield Energy: Metal only, -10 incoming attack damage per attached copy; Wonder Energy: Fairy only, block opponent attack effects but not damage; discard attached off-type Special Energy upon evolution/type change. Include only when corresponding type actually exists in auto-built decks.
- Water Veil: hand-attached Energy (not Energy Zone) removes conditions from Wailord-EX. Barrier Shrine: unsuppressed Ninetales prevents Stadium played by EITHER player.
- Energy Retrieval #126: physical Basic Energy in discard is a selectable card; spent Basic Energy Zone counters are separately selectable and consumed for up to 2 recovered Basic Energy cards.
- Permanent regression: tests/v07344-primal-clash-stage2.test.cjs. APK gate auditXY5ForApk runs Stage 1 and Stage 2; full 164-print official audit remains CI-only before Android packaging.

=== v0.69.345: XY5 Primal Clash stage 3 — physical Primal moves, passive field mechanics and Trainer choices ===
- Source baseline is the user-confirmed successful v0.69.344 GitHub APK. Current release still requires GitHub verification. Do not mark the full XY5 set done until every 164-print catalogue entry, attack, Ability and Ancient Trait is both implemented and independently audited.
- Tidal Storm (#55/#149) deals printed 150 to Active and 30 to each opposing Benched EX, THEN chooses one own Benched recipient and up to two genuine Basic or attached Special Energy to move from the attacker. No fake Energy, no discard or Energy Zone substitution. Finish mandatory choices before advancing turn or showing prizes.
- Gaia Volcano (#86/#151) gets +100 only if a Stadium is in play and physically discards that Stadium to its actual owner's discard. The Groudon prints remain GATED while Ω Barrier's global protection is unverified.
- Megaton Slam (#94/#154) optional coin: decline = plain 120, heads = +120, tails = 20 own Bench damage. Brilliant Arrow (#106/#156) = 30 per actually attached Fairy Energy across whole own board, including compatible Special Energy; Shining Wind (#105/#155) prevents Weakness on opponent's next turn.
- Miracle Guard (Aegislash) sets own team's Weakness to none while unsuppressed; Rock Wall (Rhyperior) reduces received damage by 10 AFTER Weakness/Resistance; Sap Sipper (Bouffalant) adds 40 when attacking a Grass target. Suppress appropriate Basic Abilities under Silent Lab. Do not unlock any otherwise unfinished card merely because one Ability works.
- Rough Seas: once per player's turn heal each friendly Water/Lightning by 30 (with α Recovery bonus). Scorched Earth: discard REAL Fire/Fighting Energy from physical hand to draw 2; Basic Energy available only from the separate Zone cannot pay its cost. Shrine of Memories offers real previous-Evolution attacks (printed cost still applies). Weakness Policy removes holder's Weakness. Acro Bike and Repeat Ball use actual deck/hand/discard refs and explicit choice selection.
- Ω Barrier central Item/Supporter protection has been introduced but ALL Ω Barrier cards remain EXPLICITLY GATED until older bespoke Trainer paths are independently verified. Trainer Pokémon Tools and Stadiums are not blocked by Ω Barrier, and neither are attacks. Never use a mere passive registry descriptor as evidence every targeted effect is protected.
- The automatic 30-card deck builder avoids new unsupported/no-payoff cards: Spirit Link requires matching Mega, Shield/Wonder Energy requires matching Pokémon type, Rough Seas requires Water/Lightning, Scorched Earth requires ACTUAL printed Fire/Fighting Basic Energy in the deck, Shrine of Memories requires a connected Evolution, Silent Lab avoids own Basic Ability shutdown, Repeat Ball requires same-name Pokémon duplicates. Respect explicitly retained manual choices.
- Run permanent source and generated-catalogue tests/v07345-primal-clash-stage3.test.cjs with v07343 and v07344 after immutable XY5 generation, before Android asset merging. Continue cross-era testing for Energy Zone vs physical discard, CPU target legality, Trainers, stadiums and Pokémon Tool effects. No fabricated card entries in any source ZIP.

[2026-11-21 v0.69.347 XY5 Primal Clash Stage 5 | CARD-MOVEMENT, CONDITIONAL SUPPORTERS]
- Archie/Maxie are legal only as final card in hand. Choose a real matching-type Pokémon from your own physical discard, Bench it directly (including Stage 2), then draw five real draw-deck cards. Required actual free Bench slot. Manual hand choice, no imaginary discard card.
- Teammates can be used only if one of YOUR Pokémon was knocked out during the opposing player's previous turn; choose up to two distinct actual cards from your deck, then shuffle; obey hand limit.
- Helping Hand and Electrike's Charge search FRESH Basic Energy from Classic's ordered Energy Zone, never the physical discard and never the physical 30-card deck. Search may select a matching symbol from Zone; ordinary Energy attachment turn quota is unchanged.
- Torchic Flare Bonus discards a physical Fire Energy card from hand, never the Energy Zone. Astonish returns one randomly chosen opponent-hand card to the draw deck.
- Synchro Star compares PHYSICAL Energy CARD counts including Special Energy (DCE counts as ONE card). Poison Fang has 20 between-turn damage. Pure Power places four chosen damage counters individually, preserving damage-counter immunity.
- Ω Barrier Ancient Trait cards remain gated until all bespoke old-era Trainer effects are verified, including effects applying outside generic protection hooks. DO NOT re-enable them just because a subset passes.
- Auto deck builder must not choose last-card-in-hand Aces without matching Pokémon and actual discard setup. Conditional Teammates is not generic filler.

[2026-11-21 v0.69.352 XY5 PRIMAL CLASH INDEPENDENT AUDIT | ESCAPE ROPE, EXCADRILL AND DECK SYNERGY]

- Official Primal Clash remains 164 authenticated battle-enabled prints, 217 printed attacks, 13 Abilities and 22 printed Ancient Traits. Never treat source-only Node passes as proof that a new Android APK built.
- OMEGA BARRIER and forced switches: Ω Barrier on the outgoing opponent Active prevents an opposing Item/Supporter from forcing that Active out. When the outgoing Active has NO Ω Barrier, its controller may legitimately promote an Ω Barrier Bench as replacement if the controller chooses, such as Escape Rope. The opponent playing a Trainer may NOT choose that protected Bench as its targeted effect. An opposing Escape Rope may still switch the person playing the Item's own Pokemon even if the defender Active is protected; do not cancel the whole Trainer globally. Prevent a generic catch-all snapshot from undoing allowed promotions.
- Excadrill #96 Straight Claw printed base damage is SIXTY (60), not 20. Optional real attached Energy discard adds 30; present accurate choice labels, preserving resolution before prizes.
- Auto deck-building: prioritise 1-2 matching, owned, legal Spirit Link Tools for a included M Aggron, M Gardevoir, Primal Groudon, or Primal Kyogre line before random Trainer fillers. Warn if missing. Do not add orphaned links.
- XY5 Sceptile #8 Nurture and Heal and Torchic #26 Flare Bonus require physical Energy cards IN HAND. In LuckyDex hybrid Classic30, physical Basic Energy cards are DECK-INELIGIBLE; never insert them into a 30-card deck to satisfy such prerequisites. Prefer eligible, owned Energy Search/Cilan/compatible legal Trainer cards that take actual Energy from Zone into hand; warn if none are in the deck. Energy Zone attachment alone is not a payment from hand.

- Δ Plus awards one EXTRA selectable face-down Prize only if the specific attacking Pokémon's attack DAMAGE knocked out the target (including Bench). Must use real KO source metadata, respect EX prize value, and never trigger for poison, passive effects or stale hits; retain any bonus even if attacker self-KOs in the same attack.
- Δ Wild reduces final DAMAGE by 20 only if attacker has Grass/Fire/Water/Lightning typing, after Weakness/Resistance. The Ancient Trait is NOT blocked by Ability suppression; ability-based protection is.
- Beautifly #5 Miraculous Scales blocks only damage from opponent Pokémon-EX attacks; Carbink #47 Jewel Armor protects its Bench position against own/opponent attack damage; Altaria #74 Clear Humming grants no Weakness to friendly Colorless Pokémon while unsuppressed.
- Newly mapped attack effects use correct physical target selection and damage timing: Banette Curse Deeply puts 5 damage counters without weakness; its Evolution Jammer locks opponent evolution next turn; M Gallade Unwavering Blade hits only damaged opponent Bench; M Rayquaza Emerald Break scales with actual own Bench; Unfezant Feather Dance boosts its next-turn Sky Attack; and mapped safe coin, lock, recoil and search rules retain exact effects. Both listed alternate prints share effects.

LUCKYDEX v0.69.355 — ROARING SKIES STAGE 3
========================================================================
GitHub Actions run #643 built v0.69.354 successfully. Stage 3 retains the fixed official XY6 112-print / 147-attack / 15-Ability / 12-Ancient-Trait catalogue and the immutable upstream Git blob. The newly generated catalogue still remains a build-time artifact; local Node tests are not proof of an APK build.

## Newly battle-ready prints & mechanics

- Shaymin-EX #77, #77a and #106: Set Up is an optional triggered Ability only for a Shaymin placed from the hand onto your Bench. Show a draw-to-six or decline choice. Draw only from the physical deck, obey the Classic eight-card hand ceiling and deck exhaustion. Do not trigger for deck-to-Bench effects or when the hand already has six or more. Ability suppression still applies. Sky Return already used a verified return-to-hand implementation.
- Double Dragon Energy #97: One physical Special Energy card may be attached to **Dragon-type Pokémon only**. While valid, it provides two independent flexible Energy units (Miracle:2), not all individual types simultaneously, not two physical cards and never generated Basic Energy. Both must be spendable on different typed attack requirements; other Special-Energy interactions see one physical card. On becoming non-Dragon, discard the actual attached ref. Crystal Beach and Miracle Wind interactions remain in the shared Energy logic.
- Healing Scarf #84: Heal the attached Pokémon by up to 20 **only when an Energy card enters that Pokémon from its owner's hand**, including a hand attachment through a card effect. Energy Zone, discard or deck attachments do not count. Respect any existing no-healing effects.
- Mega Turbo #86: Select a real Mega Evolution Pokémon and one eligible discarded Basic Energy, including the hybrid Basic Energy discard pool. Attach the energy; it is not an attachment from hand. If no Mega or available Basic Energy exists, do not offer a playable Trainer action.
- Trainers' Mail #92 and #92a: Inspect only the top four physical deck cards and optionally select one Trainer other than Trainers' Mail. Selecting it removes that actual card, and declining or finding no eligible card shuffles normally. The other cards return to deck; the alternate print is independently preserved.

## Deck-building / CPU rules

The existing CPU takes only legal attack and attachment actions, so Double Dragon can only be attached to Dragon Pokémon. Auto-builder reserves at most two owned/eligible Double Dragon Special Energy in decks with actual Dragon lines and excludes it from generic filler. It must not automatically add Mega Turbo without a Mega Evolution in the constructed deck. Healing Scarf needs a way to attach physical Energy from hand (for a generic deck this means Special Energy). Existing Spirit Link rules still pair their exact Mega/Primal forms.

## Not yet completed

Sky Field #89 requires a genuine **eight-slot Bench on each side** with visible/actionable slots 0–7 and a choice to discard Benched Pokémon down to five, with attached cards handled correctly, when Sky Field leaves play. The current battle UI is limited to five slots, and the LuckyDex Classic30 default normally uses three; do not enable a fake three/five-slot substitute. Other unique attacks and Abilities remain gated until each is tested.

## Regression gates

`node --test tests/v07355-roaring-skies-stage3.test.cjs` is included in `auditXY6Stage3ForApk`, downstream of both Stage 1 and Stage 2 suites and the official catalogue generator. Existing XY5, Energy, trainer and deck-quality suites were also run locally. No build success is claimed for this patch before CI verification.

# LuckyDex v0.69.357 — Roaring Skies Stage 5, remaining card interactions

## Baseline

Stage 4 (Sky Field) is the patch baseline and successfully built in GitHub Actions run #645. The new Stage 5 patch itself is not APK-verified until pushed and built. The 112 official prints remain pinned to original Git blob `5eab0c0148a5cb52dd7005628aa61c793ec0d930` and are generated during the APK build, not fabricated in the ZIP.

## Newly supported 17 prints

- Ninjask #10: Afterimage Assault searches up to two physical Ninjask from the draw deck and Benches them using player-selected slots, respecting available Bench space.
- Shedinja #11: Cursed Rain places a damage counter on every opposing Pokémon, then switches with a friendly Benched Pokémon; Hopeless Scream was already supported.
- Deoxys #33: Close Encounter draws two; Overdrive Smash gives only its next use +60 on the next own turn, not the current attack.
- Gallade-EX #34/#99: Piercing Prizes deals 50 plus 20 for each of the attacker's *remaining* Prize cards (five-card Classic variant), not opponent prizes.
- Jirachi #42: Diminutive Desire selects one from the top seven physical deck cards and shuffles the rest; Doom Desire discards all actual attached Energy and schedules a knockout of the defending Pokémon at the *end of the opponent's next turn*, following that Pokémon by UID if it moves.
- Dragonite #51: Max Wind on evolving from hand offers optional healing of one chosen friendly Pokémon; it does not fire on evolution from deck, and standard Ability suppression applies.
- Shelgon #56: Exoskeleton continuously reduces attacking damage to Shelgon by ten after matchup, respecting suppression.
- Latios-EX #58/#101: Fast Raid uses the existing permitted first-turn attack path; Light Pulse protects against opposing *attack effects only*, never damage. A cross-era engine fix distinguishes this from all-damage attack guards.
- M Latios-EX #59/#102: Sonic Ace discards two attached Energy and deals 120 damage to a selected opposing Pokémon, with correct bench targeting.
- Hydreigon-EX #62/#103: Dragon Road continuously reduces allied Dragon retreat costs by two only while a Stadium is in play; no reduction for non-Dragon or when suppressed.
- Zekrom #64: Energy Stream attaches one genuinely discarded Basic Energy, never pulling from the fresh Energy Zone.
- Swablu #73: Bind Wound heals a chosen friendly Pokémon by twenty; it is not hardwired to the attacker.
- Pidove #78: Homing Pidove inspects the actual top physical card and offers a genuine optional shuffle.

## Coverage and safeguards

This is a Stage 5 implementation, not a claim that all Roaring Skies cards are battle ready. Gated cards with unimplemented mechanisms remain excluded from CPU decks and autobuilds. Alternate prints are preserved as unique collection IDs and receive exactly the same printed attack logic.

`tests/v07357-roaring-skies-stage5.test.cjs` uses injected print-faithful fixtures, checks all 17 enabled prints, their effect validation and attack/Ability outcomes, and runs under the `auditXY6Stage5ForApk` Gradle preflight after the Stage 4 audit and official catalogue generator. Targeted XY6+XY5+deck-quality suites have been run locally. The new test includes effect-only protection vs attack damage, Dragon Road team scope, and independent Bench, discard and Energy ledgers. The full APK build must still be verified on GitHub.

## Termux

git add source

git push origin main

# LuckyDex v0.69.358 — XY Roaring Skies Stage 6 (verified interactions)

## Baseline and coverage

GitHub Actions #646 completed successfully for v0.69.357 Stage 5. This ZIP builds on that source, retains the official pinned 112-print Roaring Skies catalogue (Git blob `5eab0c0148a5cb52dd7005628aa61c793ec0d930`) and enables **10 additional original/alternate prints**: #14, #23, #29, #48, #52, #53, #63, #80, and Wally #94/#107 (**10 prints total**).

### Card-by-card

- **Fletchinder #14 — Peck Off** removes every attached Pokémon Tool on the defending Active *before* damage calculation. A pre-damage effect tag avoids the old error where Hard Charm or other Tool protection reduced the hit before being removed.
- **Zapdos #23 — Raging Thunder** deals 120 damage to the defending Active, then precisely 40 damage to a player-chosen friendly Benched Pokémon. It cannot silently damage the Active instead.
- **Xatu #29 — Future Sight** allows choosing either player's physical deck and ordering its actual top five cards individually; **Stressful Eye** reveals the opposing hand and lets the attacker select a Trainer card to discard, rather than choosing randomly or asking the wrong player.
- **Klefki #48 — Look for Keys** reveals physical draw-deck cards in order until the first Item, takes that Item, and shuffles all other revealed cards back. Without an Item it safely reshuffles the deck without manufacturing one or freezing.
- **Dragonite #52 — Wrapped in Wind** selects zero, one or two actual *Basic Energy cards in hand*; multiple colors can be attached, optional choices may be declined, and no fresh Zone Energy is generated.
- **Altaria #53 — Song of Echoes** shuffles its owner's hand into their draw deck and draws based on both players' occupied Bench slots (including Sky Field's expanded eight-slot Benches, subject to the Classic hand cap).
- **Reshiram #63 — Turboblaze** is an Active-only, once-per-turn Ability that attaches a Fire Energy *from hand* to a chosen Dragon Pokémon. It is not offered when no Fire Energy is in hand, does not use unspent Zone Energy, and signals correct hand-attachment triggers.
- **Unfezant #80 — Strong Winds** shuffles all players' attached Tool/Special Energy cards and Basic Energy cards back into their respective physical decks without relocating or discarding the Pokémon; no false Energy Zone refill.
- **Wally #94/#107** selects a real non-EX Pokémon on the board and a compatible evolution from the physical deck; this bypasses only the usual first-turn/newly-played timing restrictions, not evolution prohibitions. Both prints are playable. The autobuilder avoids Wally unless the selected 30-card deck has a compatible parent/evolution pair.

The gating system remains authoritative: any printed card still lacking an effect is **not** enabled in battle or auto-generated CPU decks. Stage 6 is not the final all-112 print audit. In particular, Banette Tool Concealment, Absol Cursed Eyes, Togekiss Serene Grace, Electrode Energy Bomb, and Wide Lens require further integration.

## Tests and build

`tests/v07358-roaring-skies-stage6.test.cjs` exercises physical draw/discard choices, turn ordering, before-damage Tool removal, dual-Bench draw, multiple hand-Energy colors, Turboblaze activation, Wally first-turn evolution/EX restrictions, and both-side attachment resets. The `auditXY6Stage6ForApk` Gradle task runs after all five older XY6 stages and pinned catalogue generation and is tied to the APK prebuild.

Prior to bundling the patch, the Roaring Skies Stages 1–6 plus deck-quality suite passed 68, failed 0 and skipped 3 catalogue-dependent tests; Stage 6's expanded direct suite passed 16/16. The APK build has not yet run for v0.69.358.

## Termux installation

```sh
cd ~/lucky-dex
unzip -o /sdcard/Download/LuckyDex_patch_v0.69.358_ROARING_SKIES_STAGE6.zip -d source
git add source
git commit -m "LuckyDex v0.69.358 Roaring Skies Stage 6 interactions"
git push origin main
```

  Pokémon (one counter = 10 damage). Do not classify it as Bench attack
  damage; Wide Lens, Weakness and Resistance do not multiply counters.
  Its printed self-switch follows the placement.
- Banette #31 Tool Concealment blanks the EFFECTS of Tools globally, but
  does not detach them or stop effects that depend only on a Tool existing.
  Guard older hard-coded effects: Head Ringer adds no attack Energy cost;
  Rocky Helmet reflects no damage; Unown E EQUIP adds no HP; Plasma Badge
  grants no Team Plasma effect while Banette suppresses its text. When the
  Ability is suppressed/removed, attached Tools resume functioning normally.
- The independent suite tests actual battle-state interactions and source
  descriptors; 23 local suites: 249 passed, 0 failed, 7 generated-catalogue
  dependent skips. `auditXY6IndependentForApk` must run after the 112-print
  catalogue generation and after the Stage 8/Completion tests.
- The next set is NOT blocked by source coverage, but is blocked by an
  unverified v0.69.362 APK until GitHub completes its CI run successfully.
- Termux:
    cd ~/lucky-dex

LUCKYDEX v0.69.364 — XY ANCIENT ORIGINS STAGE 2 (θ ANCIENT TRAITS)
========================================================================
[CURRENT] Stage 1 v0.69.363 successfully built on GitHub (run #652).
[CURRENT] Stage 2 changes are locally tested; GitHub APK has not yet built.
- θ Stop: opposing Pokémon Ability effects done to a θ Stop Pokémon
  are prevented. Own Abilities, attacks and Trainer effects are not
  blocked. Ancient Traits are independent of Pokémon Abilities; ability
  suppression must not disable θ Stop itself. Guard old direct Ability
  mutations as well as generic targeted damage/status effects.
- θ Double: a θ Double Pokémon has a capacity of TWO physical Pokémon
  Tools, held as tool + extraTools. Both must be inspectable, removable,
  and discarded to the correct owner. Ordinary Pokémon still have one.
  Banette suppresses both Tool effects but does not detach either Tool.
- θ Max: on evolving/reverting INTO the printed θ Max Mega or Primal
  Pokémon, heal inherited damage entirely unless healing is blocked.
  Do not remove attached Energy/Tools or treat θ Max as an Ability.
- θ Max reprints xy7-96, xy7-97, xy7-98 reuse their exactly matching
  older Tidal Storm, Gaia Volcano and Emerald Break handlers. Kyogre,
  Groudon, Rayquaza Spirit Links from prior sets also cover reprints,
  including automatic deck-building synergy and Tool Concealment.
- 10 formerly gated θ print identities now enabled in source: #11,
  #15, #18, #32, #43, #50, #92, #96, #97, #98.
- θ-printed cards #8, #21, #35, #67, #85 remain gated due to OTHER
  unfinished printed attacks or Abilities. All other incomplete XY7
  cards, continuous Special Energy and global Stadium/Supporter effects
  remain gated rather than pretending fully supported.
- Stage 2 regression: 132 passed, 0 failed, 6 catalogue-dependent skips
  in selected cross-set tests (Primal Clash + Roaring Skies + XY7).
  GitHub runs both Stage 1 and Stage 2 checks after catalog generation.

========================================================================
LUCKYDEX v0.69.366 — XY ANCIENT ORIGINS STAGE 4 (STADIUMS / ENERGY)
========================================================================
[CURRENT] v0.69.366 implements six physical XY7 prints across five
mechanics; the GitHub APK build of this patch is not yet verified.
- Faded Town #73: between-turn damage is two counters on EACH Mega
  Evolution Pokémon of either player, including Benched Pokémon.
  Counters are not damage from an attack; replacement ends the effect.
- Forest of Giant Plants #74: Grass Pokémon (determined from effective
  type) may evolve on the first turn and the turn they entered play.
  It does not globally remove evolution requirements or non-Grass timing.
- Hex Maniac #75/#75a: one Supporter blocks ALL Pokémon Abilities
  through the opponent's following turn, including subsequently played
  Pokémon and abilities used from the discard pile. The block ends on
  the original player's next turn. Ancient Traits are not Abilities.
- Dangerous Energy #82: only Darkness hosts; physical Special Energy
  provides one Darkness unit and places two counters on opposing
  attacking EX when its Darkness Active host takes attack damage, even
  if knocked out. Counter placement is not attacking damage.
- Flash Energy #83: only Lightning hosts; physical Special Energy
  provides one Lightning unit and removes Weakness during damage
  calculations, including Bench Weakness where Wide Lens applies.
- Moving #82/#83 to an invalid host causes a real discard; preserving
  ownership/identity is mandatory. No Energy Zone substitution is made
  for these two physical Special Energy cards.
- Autobuilder: avoid #82/#83 in incompatible typing strategies; avoid
  Faded Town in Mega-dependent plans and require Grass evolution
  synergy for Forest. Keep players' manually constructed decks intact.
- Eight new direct battle tests passed locally; the focused older-set
  and deck/energy suites also passed. Catalogue-generated/Android tests
  still require GitHub APK CI. The 101-print XY7 set is not yet complete.
- Termux:
    cd ~/lucky-dex
    unzip -o /sdcard/Download/LuckyDex_patch_v0.69.366_ANCIENT_ORIGINS_STAGE4.zip -d source
    git add source
    git commit -m "LuckyDex v0.69.366 Ancient Origins Stage 4 rules"
    git push origin main
